# 67: Chapter 67: Lillie

Dylan watched as Kiyo left with his sister and couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

"Let's go, sis. We're never coming back to this trash gym again!"

"There's absolutely no spirit of a true Trainer here! Pah, pah, pah! What bad luck!"

"Disgusting! Absolutely disgusting!!"

Kiyo cursed as he quickened his pace.

Luna turned back and gave Dylan an apologetic look, to which Dylan responded with a smile, signaling that it was fine.

"Pfft, haha! Gym Leader, that big guy just now looked like he was about to throw a punch."

The receptionist and Selene also approached from the spectator seats, with the receptionist grinning widely.

"You were amazing, Gym Leader!" Selene was in awe.

In fact, the Gym battle just now didn't end in the usual way. After Machamp was defeated, Kiyo simply broke down and refused to continue.

For a Trainer, winning without even fighting was definitely something to be proud of.

"It's nothing," Dylan chuckled, ruffling Selene's hair.

"Selene, make sure to watch and learn. Someday, you'll have to take down the Champion."

"Gym Leader!" Selene's fair cheeks puffed up as she heard Dylan bring this up again.

"Haha~, I'm heading to the backyard. If any challengers show up, just let me know."

Dylan smiled and left a note for the two girls before turning and heading towards the backyard.

Truth be told, Dylan was quite satisfied with the power of his Dark-type team in today's first experiment.

When it comes to messing with opponents' minds, Dark-types are really effective. It's a shame that as a Gym Leader, he can't switch Pokémon freely during battles.

Otherwise, he could have made a switch after Sableye's Parting Shot to bring Incineroar back with another Intimidate, making the opponent's Pokémon crumble under the pressure. That would be satisfying!

The cycle of life and death continues, but we find joy while they break.

"There's still room for improvement. I should consider adding Zoroark to the team—its Illusion ability has so much potential."

Dylan pondered as he thought about how to refine his team. Although Gym Leaders technically can't switch Pokémon at will, moves like Parting Shot that allow for tactical switches are fair game in official Gym battles, offering some strategic flexibility.

"Once the Dark-type team is fully assembled, maybe I should rotate the lineups?"

Dylan considered it and felt it could be done.

Monday could be the Poison team, Tuesday the Dark team, Wednesday back to Poison? Or maybe he could let challengers spin a wheel to decide which team he'd use.

As for whether this would cause challengers who came prepared for Poison-types to be crushed… well, that would be their bad luck.

Besides, as a Trainer, learning to adapt to any situation is part of growing up!

"I'll have to ask Dad to prepare the Pokémon for me."

Dylan reached the backyard and thought for a moment before making a video call to his dad.

**Ring... ring... ring...**

The video call was answered after just three rings, revealing the handsome face of a middle-aged man.

"Hey? Dylan, what made you call me today?"

In the video, his dad, dressed in a floral shirt and beach shorts, with sunglasses hanging from his collar, smiled and waved at Dylan.

His arms were also loaded with various shopping bags.

"I'm out shopping with your mom and your sister."

He turned the camera, showing a high-end clothing store where a mature woman and a blonde girl in a princess dress were picking out clothes.

Dylan's eyes flickered slightly when he saw the blonde girl's profile.

"Hey, honey, Lillie, Dylan is on a video call."

His dad called out to the two women.

Lillie?

Dylan raised an eyebrow—what a coincidence.

Lillie, the girl from Generation VII, the young lady of the Aether Foundation. She ran away from home to protect Nebby and escape her mother's control. Later, she traveled across the ruins to discover Nebby's origins.

In the game, Selene is considered Lillie's shining light. From the moment they first met, the two had a fateful encounter.

Later, when Lillie was in despair after Necrozma forcibly fused with Solgaleo (or Lunala), it was Selene who saved her.

"Oh? Dylan's calling?"

Upon hearing this, the mature woman happily walked over and quickly took the phone.

"Mom," Dylan greeted warmly.

Compared to his playful dad, Dylan was much more well-behaved in front of his mother, Amelia.

"Dylan, I've seen the news about Kanto recently. You've done an amazing job," Amelia smiled with pride and warmth.

"I even told the elders about your accomplishments, and they're very happy for you. Your father and I were planning to visit the Meteor Village in Hoenn after our trip to Alola, but we happened to run into Lillie."

"Oh, right, Dylan, this is your sister, Lillie. Isn't she adorable? She's such a sweet child."

Amelia waved to Lillie, who was standing obediently nearby, and Lillie stepped into the frame, her pure, wide eyes curiously observing Dylan.

He looked exactly like the photos and videos her foster parents had shown her—a very handsome and refined young man.

"Hello. I'm Lillie," she said softly, trying to suppress her nervousness.

This was truly surreal. Lillie could hardly believe it. She had fled Aether Paradise to escape her mother, Lusamine.

But when she left, she had very little food and money, and she wasn't skilled at anything. Although she could survive by eating wild berries, life was still very difficult, not to mention that Lusamine was still sending people to search for her.

Then she happened to run into Amelia and Dylan's father, Itto, who were enjoying the scenery in the wild. Seeing such a young girl in such a miserable state, Amelia's maternal instincts kicked in.

And after hearing Lillie's fabricated story of being kicked out by her family, Amelia was furious.

And that was that.

Amelia and Itto temporarily took Lillie in, and Amelia insisted on making Lillie her goddaughter. At first, Lillie refused, but she wasn't strong-willed, and under Amelia's gentle persuasion, she eventually agreed.

"Hello, Lillie. It's quite the surprise to meet you this way. When I get some time to visit Alola, I'll bring you a gift," Dylan said with a smile.

"No need, really," Lillie quickly replied.

"I insist. It's a token of affection from your big brother. Or are you saying you don't accept me as your brother?"

"That's not it…"

"Then you can't refuse, right? We're family now, and family can rely on each other. It's perfectly normal to give gifts among family, right?"

Dylan's gentle smile grew as he spoke.

"…" Lillie bit her lip in silence, but her eyes shimmered with emotion.

Since her biological father went missing, her mother had become increasingly obsessed with studying Ultra Beasts. Her mother's personality had become more and more self-centered and obsessive, making the once loving and dependable mother seem more and more distant and frightening.

She had rarely felt the warmth of family.

But over the past few days, with Amelia's meticulous care and Itto's reliability, and now the kindness from this brother she hadn't even met in person, Lillie's heart was deeply touched.

"Thank you, big brother…" Lillie lowered her head and whispered.

"You're welcome. We're family now," Dylan smiled and nodded.

"I've always wanted a little sister when I was younger, but my parents just couldn't do it, which was always a regret for me."

"Who would've thought I'd end up with a god-sister now? I guess that's good enough," Dylan joked lightly.

"You cheeky boy," his dad laughed and scolded him.

"When you get the chance, come to Alola. We need to have a proper meal together."

"I know, I know. I'll see when I can take some time off from the League."

Dylan sighed; Alola was quite a distance from Kanto.

"You'd better take a long vacation. Dylan, the elders said that Zinnia has officially become the Lorekeeper and left the Meteor Village. We should go bless Zinnia together," his mother, Amelia, added.

Lillie listened curiously. She knew her foster father was quite impressive since he had proudly told her that he was the wealthiest man in both Kanto and Johto…

Holding a 54% stake in Silph Co., one of the world's largest companies.

But it seemed that her foster mother's background wasn't simple either? Lorekeeper? Meteor Village? She had never heard of those…

But they sounded extraordinary.

However, Lillie remained obediently quiet, not asking any questions.

"…Alright."

Dylan was silent for a moment before nodding.

Meteor Village…

# 68: Chapter 68: Aster And Zinnia

After ending the video call with his parents, Dylan fell into a contemplative silence.

He instinctively recalled the older sister who always wore a smile on her face when he was young.

Aster...

"So, it seems that no matter the world, scum always exists," Dylan mused, rubbing his temples with a hint of helplessness.

The "Meteor Village" mentioned by Amelia was actually the name of a village, but it was no ordinary village.

In the Hoenn region, there is a famous wild area known as Meteor Falls, which is on par with Johto's Mt. Silver and Sinnoh's Mt. Coronet.

Meteor Falls is also one of the habitats for the preliminary form of the quasi-legendary Dragon-type Pokémon, Bagon. However, the wild Pokémon deep within Meteor Falls are of high level and strength, so it's rare for Trainers to venture deep inside.

But what few outsiders know is that deep within Meteor Falls is a village called Meteor Village.

The inhabitants of Meteor Village, known as the Draconid People, have had a heritage spanning thousands of years. The Draconid People are an ancient clan that worships Rayquaza and are generally experts in Dragon-type Pokémon.

For two thousand years, the Draconid People have maintained their traditions, making them one of the most enduring cultures in the world. This is absolute, as even the Pokémon League was only established a little over a century ago, and the Orange Archipelago has only existed for eight hundred years.

However, as of now, there are not many Draconid People left in Meteor Village. Over the long years, most of them have moved to live in the outside world.

For example, Dylan's mother, Amelia, is one of them. Eighteen years ago, Amelia left Meteor Village, and soon after met Dylan's father, Itto. The two quickly fell in love and married.

However, Amelia still keeps in touch with the elders of Meteor Village and occasionally brings Itto and Dylan back to visit.

It was during these visits that Dylan met the sisters Aster and Zinnia.

But even in the Pokémon world, not everything is kind and beautiful. In fact, Aster had already died many years ago.

Not due to an accident, but due to human greed and desire, Aster tragically perished in the Embedded Tower in Johto.

The most ironic thing is that the Embedded Tower were actually built by the Draconid People for Rayquaza, but it became the resting place of Aster, the Lorekeeper of the Draconid People.

"Zinnia ended up becoming the Lorekeeper after all..." Dylan thought, gazing at the nearby Pokémon, such as his Arbok and Drapion, as they trained.

Zinnia, a character not present in the anime but quite significant in the games and special episodes, is known for her formidable strength.

"So, mixed-type worlds are really troublesome," Dylan said, feeling a headache coming on.

In the special episodes, Zinnia is actually a self-proclaimed "Lorekeeper," a title not recognized by the other Draconid People.

However, in this world, Zinnia is recognized as the Lorekeeper by the Draconid People, clearly resembling the game's version of Zinnia, while the death of Aster at the Embedded Tower is a story from the special episodes.

"Well, I'll ask Zinnia about it when I visit Meteor Village."

Dylan shook his head, deciding not to dwell on the matter. Aster's death had occurred nine years ago, and Dylan was too young at the time to understand the specifics.

He would ask Zinnia properly during his next visit to Meteor Village.

As night fell, Dylan entered the Growth Simulation World once again.

In the Indigo Plateau, Dylan entered the fourth room, where he saw a young man in a red outfit and cape with red hair, none other than Lance.

But unlike the cold and aloof Lance from the real world, this version of Lance had a much more "cheerful" demeanor.

"Yes, you've arrived, Dylan!"

Lance greeted Dylan with a smile, his face radiating warmth.

Dylan tilted his head slightly.

"I am one of the Elite Four! Dragon Master Lance!"

"Do you know? Dragons are legendary and sacred creatures. Although they are difficult to capture, their strength is unparalleled if nurtured properly!"

Lance declared with increasing fervor, raising his hands in proclamation.

This sounded... very familiar.

Dylan remained expressionless, not expecting to hear Lance's declaration again after already hearing it from the Champion in reality.

Alright, alright!

"They are strong and powerful; mere tricks won't work against them! Well then, it's about time to begin!"

Lance looked at Dylan, taking out a Poké Ball with a grin.

"Or should you just turn tail and run now, Dylan?"

"No, I'm going to beat you."

Dylan suddenly smiled, taking out an Ultra Ball.

"Articuno! Time to work!"

And then...

"Wow~ So strong! I still can't believe my Dragon team lost!"

Lance knelt on the ground, muttering in disbelief.

No, you must believe it; you were defeated by my Articuno.

Dylan was somewhat speechless. The level 58 Gyarados, 56 Dragonair, 56 Dragonair, 60 Aerodactyl, and 62 Dragonite had been easily handled by Articuno after a few High Potions.

It seemed that Elite Four Lance had finally accepted the reality and stood up with a smile.

"From now on, you are the Champion of the Pokémon League! Oh, wait, actually, you must defeat one more person first."

"That Trainer is Blue. He defeated us Elite Four before you, and now he is the new Champion of the Pokémon League."

Lance suddenly remembered, his face showing some frustration, as if he couldn't describe his current mood.

In a short time, they had been defeated in turn by two people.

"Well then, go ahead."

Nodding at Lance, Dylan proceeded to the final Champion room.

The Champion room was much more spacious and brighter than the Elite Four rooms. As Dylan pushed the door open, his gaze finally settled on a throne at the top.

At that moment, a young man in a black fitted shirt and purple pants, with a spiky brown hair and a handsome, smug face, was seated on the throne.

It was Blue!

"Yo~ Dylan!"

Blue squeezed a Poké Ball and slowly stood up, looking down at Dylan from above.

Dylan raised an eyebrow. So it was Blue after all, though he hadn't encountered him throughout his journey.

"Dylan, you've arrived~ You finally came! Hahaha~"

Blue descended the steps, his handsome, defiant face wearing an unmistakable air of arrogance and mockery.

"It's laughable, really. As a rival, you have no competitive value whatsoever."

Upon reaching the battlefield, Blue tightened his grip on the Poké Ball, lifting his head slightly with a sharp and arrogant smile.

Dylan watched Blue's performance with an expressionless face. He had tried before; these 'NPCs' would simply ignore him and continue their scripted lines.

"Do you know what this means, Dylan? While collecting data and searching for perfect Pokémon, I've assembled the strongest team! And now, I stand at the peak of the Pokémon League!"

Blue spread his arms in a welcoming gesture, his arrogant tone impressive.

"Dylan, do you know what this means?"

"Alright! Let me tell you! This Lord! The Strongest in the World! Stronger than anyone else!"

Blue grinned widely, throwing his Poké Ball with a flourish.

Champion Blue, I challenge you!

And then...

"Damn it! Is it really ending like this? Losing in a full-out battle?!! I finally reached the top of the Pokémon League!"

Blue collapsed on the ground in exhaustion, pounding the floor in frustration.

"Ugh... Has my reign come to an end? It shouldn't be like this! Why... why did I lose? My training... it was flawless..."

Blue fell into self-doubt.

Dylan savored the sight of this defeated figure, pondering if Blue would react similarly in reality after being defeated.

Speaking of which, defeating Blue would mean completing the Kanto region. Soon, he would be able to register his Champion team in the Hall of Fame.

Zapdos or Articuno— which bird should he choose?

Dylan pondered this.

(The End of This Chapter)

# 69: Chapter 69: Articuno

Inside the Hall of Fame.

Dylan stood in front of a computer, with images of various Pokémon displayed on the screen.

Articuno, Zapdos, Meowscarada, Dragonair, Incineroar, and Glimmet—a total of six Pokémon.

Moltres, however, had been stored away by Dylan. Although Moltres was good, Dylan hadn't planned to take it with him from the start.

But Articuno and Zapdos...

'Ding~ Congratulations, Host, for becoming the Kanto Region Champion and entering the Kanto Hall of Fame for the first time.'

'The Host may choose one Pokémon from the Champion team to materialize.'

The system's light screen appeared in front of Dylan as expected.

"In terms of strength, Zapdos is definitely better."

Dylan ignored the light screen, his gaze shifting back and forth between Zapdos and Articuno.

It was a tough decision. Dylan knew very well that in terms of pure strength, Zapdos was certainly stronger than Articuno.

With excellent special attack and speed, and a Flying + Electric type combination that offers better resistances than Articuno's Flying + Ice, especially since Articuno has the fatal weakness of being four times weak to Rock.

But Articuno is an Ice type!

"Building a Hail team to take on Lance is essential." Dylan pondered for a moment, his gaze finally settling on Articuno.

Articuno's strength was decent enough, being one of the more capable Ice-type Pokémon.

Against Lance, a Hail team was definitely the best choice.

The main issue was that Dylan didn't know the exact composition of Lance's current team, but no matter what, a Hail team would be the right match against Lance.

"I'll look for the other three legendary birds in reality later. No, wait, if we follow the special storyline, Team Rocket will capture the three legendary birds eventually. I can just take them from there."

Dylan suddenly thought of this. After all, Team Rocket was bound to be defeated sooner or later, so why not take the three legendary birds?

I should keep an eye on the clashes between Team Rocket and the Pokémon League; they're bound to have a final showdown.

"System, I choose Articuno."

"Host has chosen Articuno, do you confirm?"

"Confirm."

"Selection successful. The Host may now take Articuno out of the Growth Simulation."

The system's light screen flickered quickly.

"The Kanto Region has been cleared. The Host may choose to go to other regions and can return to the Kanto Region at any time."

"Finally, a new map is unlocked." Dylan opened the options for other regions.

Johto, Hoenn, Sinnoh, Unova, Kalos, Alola, Galar, Paldea.

Since becoming a Champion and entering the Hall of Fame allows you to bring a Pokémon into the real world, there's no doubt that it's best to choose a region where you can capture a legendary Pokémon before becoming Champion.

"Sinnoh is good, the Arceus isn't a joke, but..."

Dylan's gaze hesitated between Alola and Galar.

In Alola, he could get Necrozma; in Galar, he could get Eternatus.

There's no doubt that these two regions offer the best value.

Whether it's Ultra Necrozma or Eternamax Eternatus, both are top-tier legendaries!

If he could obtain either of these legendary Pokémon, wouldn't this world be at Dylan's mercy?

The world is too small for me to conquer alone!

But actually, Sinnoh might be the best choice. Why? Because Sinnoh has Arceus!

However, to obtain Arceus, you need the Azure Flute, which isn't available in the Diamond/Pearl/Platinum versions.

In the remakes, Brilliant Diamond/Shining Pearl, you can get the Azure Flute, but only if you have a save file from Legends: Arceus, and you must have completed the "Main Mission 27: The Pokémon Called the God."

"System, why isn't the Hisui region listed?"

"Hisui is a hidden region; the Host must explore it independently."

Seeing the text on the system screen, Dylan felt a bit speechless.

Hisui is actually the ancient Sinnoh region, so there's no need to guess; this exploration is definitely related to the Sinnoh region.

But compared to exploring the Hisui region, Dylan still thought that Necrozma and Eternatus were more appealing.

"Let's evolve Dragonair into Dragonite first, then decide which region to choose."

Dylan pondered for a moment and decided not to rush into choosing a region. Instead, he stayed in Kanto for a while longer.

Dragonair was already at level 53, and with focused training, its level was rising quickly.

Early the next morning.

Dylan rubbed his head as he sat up, appreciating one of the benefits of the Growth Simulation—never lacking in sleep quality.

After getting up and washing up, Dylan went to the backyard.

"Come out."

Dylan took out an Ultra Ball and opened it.

With a flash of white light, an ice-blue bird Pokémon appeared in the air, flapping its wings slowly as it hovered. As it flew, tiny ice crystals gently fell, accompanied by a cool breeze—an exceptionally beautiful sight.

One of the legendary bird Pokémon, Articuno!

'Beep~ Articuno, Ice/Flying type, Freeze Pokémon

Ability: Snow Cloak

Moves: Blizzard, Hurricane, Hail, Ice Beam, Roost, Freeze-Dry, Tailwind, Reflect, Protect...

Description: A legendary bird Pokémon that controls ice. It is said that it will snow when Articuno flies by because its wings cool the air.'

Its beautiful blue feathers shimmered with a faint blue glow, almost translucent, with a crown of three deep blue, diamond-shaped feathers on its head. Its eyes were red, and the most striking feature was its tail feathers.

The long tail feathers were deep blue, swaying gracefully as Articuno flew, elegant and stunning.

"So beautiful."

Dylan watched as Articuno flew gracefully in the sky, nodding in satisfaction.

Indeed, among the three legendary birds, only Articuno had the looks.

Moltres' wings looked like roasted chicken wings, and although Zapdos was strong, its spiky feathers made it look like a raggedy bird.

"Articuno."

"Yiao~"

The ethereal and melodious cry echoed in the sky. Articuno, who was flying around the backyard, heard Dylan's call, turned around, and slowly landed, folding its wings as it stood before Dylan, tilting its head to look at him.

"Hello, Articuno. Do you recognize me?"

"Yiao~"

Articuno nodded in a human-like manner. The familiar bond wasn't a lie; it had the memories of being captured by Dylan and battling for him.

"My Articuno is really beautiful."

Dylan approached Articuno, and a gentle chill immediately enveloped him. Dylan gently stroked Articuno's feathers, which were soft, cool, and had an incredible texture.

A smile spread across Dylan's lips. From today onwards, he was also a Trainer with a legendary Pokémon!

"Yiya~"

Articuno understood that Dylan was complimenting it. Its red eyes sparkled with joy, and it closed them in a smile, leaning its head down to nuzzle against Dylan's cheek affectionately.

"You're still like a big kid," Dylan said with a chuckle, rubbing Articuno's head.

A typical Articuno is about 1.7 meters tall, but Dylan's Articuno seemed to be at least 2 meters tall, a head taller than Dylan.

With a wingspan of at least four or five meters, this Articuno was exceptionally well-developed.

As Dylan continued to pet it, Articuno seemed even happier, snuggling its head into Dylan's chest affectionately.

It seems to have a very affectionate and playful nature.

Dylan's smile deepened.

Stare~!

Not far away, Meowscarada's jewel-like eyes were fixed on Dylan and Articuno, its face clearly showing displeasure.

This oversized bird is trying to steal my trainer's affection!!!

(End of Chapter)

# 70: Chapter 70: Causing More Trouble

"Dylan~ Huh?"

Ilene arrived at the backyard, only to find that, unlike usual, Dylan wasn't the first thing she noticed.

But he should be in the backyard, right?

Ilene looked around in confusion. Drapion and the others were all engaged in their regular training.

Then Ilene spotted Meowscarada sitting on the ground, leaning its forehead against a tree in a dejected manner.

"Meow-ha?"

"Meow~ Meow! Meow Meow!!"

Hearing Ilene's voice, Meowscarada snapped out of its daze and rushed over to her, meowing excitedly.

Ilene tilted her head slightly, nodding as she smiled softly.

"Meow Meow~"

Seeing this, Meowscarada's gem-like pupils filled with anticipation. Did this woman agree? That's great! That smug bird is done for! As long as I make a small move...

"Emmm... Meow-ha, I can't understand what you're saying, though."

Ilene spoke with a gentle smile.

"??"

Meowscarada froze, then mechanically turned around and returned to its spot under the tree, leaning its forehead against it again, falling back into a state of despair.

Please don't disturb me.jpg

"Emmm..." Ilene curiously watched Meowscarada.

"Yiao~"

A clear, melodious cry suddenly rang out, catching Ilene's attention.

A graceful blue figure slowly appeared in her line of sight, its body shimmering with a faint, beautiful glow, and its long, flowing tail feathers swayed elegantly in the wind.

"What's that?"

Ilene's eyes widened slightly. Could it be the legendary Articuno?!

There was no mistaking it. Because of Dylan, Ilene had seen the national Pokédex many times, and the legendary bird Pokémon was indeed one of Kanto's legends.

She had even watched some videos of Articuno, captured by travelers who had encountered it by chance.

But doesn't Articuno usually appear only in snowy environments or places like snow-covered mountains?

Meowscarada also snapped its head around, its cat-like pupils becoming razor-sharp!

Articuno's figure drew closer, finally circling above the backyard twice before gracefully landing.

"Dylan?!!"

At this point, Ilene clearly saw Dylan on Articuno's back, her face lighting up with surprise and delight.

"Ilene, you're here." Dylan jumped down from Articuno, smiling as he looked at her.

"Sorry, this little one insisted on taking me for a flight."

Dylan gently stroked Articuno's smooth, cool feathers, clearly satisfied with his first experience of riding a Pokémon.

Although sitting on Articuno was a bit chilly, overall, it was a great experience—Articuno's flight was very stable.

Articuno affectionately nuzzled Dylan's cheek.

Meowscarada gazed gloomily at the pure-hearted Articuno. That smug bird!

"This Articuno is...?"

Ilene curiously approached Articuno, wow!! It really is Articuno!

"I caught it last night. Beautiful, isn't it?" Dylan asked with a grin.

"Amazing~ Dylan, you're incredible!! Articuno has such an ethereal aura."

Ilene was in awe, never expecting her childhood friend to have captured a legendary bird Pokémon.

"Yiao~"

Articuno also understood that the human female in front of it was complimenting it, so it gracefully lifted its head, puffed out its chest, and let out a soft cry.

"This little one says it appreciates your compliment," Dylan translated.

"Wow~ So polite! Can I touch it?" Ilene's eyes lit up.

"Yiya~"

"Sure, but it says not to touch its tail feathers."

"Okay~ Ooh, it feels a bit cold. The Pokédex says Articuno can make it snow just by flying, is that true?"

"If Articuno wants to, it can."

Dylan replied with a smile, earning another round of amazement from Ilene.

As she watched the harmonious scene of the two humans and Articuno, Meowscarada felt a sense of melancholy, as if a heavy weight was pressing down on its head.

Beep~ Beep~

Suddenly, Dylan's phone rang. He glanced at it in surprise but answered it nonetheless.

"Hello?"

"Hello~ Hero Gym Leader, what are you up to?"

A playful, lively voice came through, full of teasing.

"Just thinking about where to go next. Why did you suddenly call me, Miss Leah?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Although they hadn't seen each other for six days, Leah's energetic voice was exactly the same.

"Hmm~ Hehe~ Because Miss Leah misses the Hero Gym Leader!" Leah giggled.

"If you're just talking nonsense, I'll hang up now," Dylan said with a chuckle.

"Huh? No way! How can you hang up on a beautiful girl's call? We have a friendship forged through life and death!"

Miss Leah protested.

"So, what's the real reason you called, Miss Leah? And don't say it's because you're bored."

Dylan rolled his eyes. He could tell with his toes that Leah had a reason for calling him.

Although they occasionally communicated via text, Leah only called when there was something important.

"Tch~ I found a Team Rocket base."

"For that, you should contact the League and Officer Jenny."

Dylan thought for a moment and replied to Leah.

"Aren't you part of the League?" Leah retorted.

Dylan was momentarily speechless. She was right—he was a legal Gym Leader in Kanto's League, a part of the League.

"Of all the League higher-ups I know, you're the only one, Dylan." Leah was straightforward.

"Where is it? What's the scale?" Dylan pondered.

"I'm in Celadon City right now. I've infiltrated this base; it's huge! Much larger than any other Team Rocket base I've seen before." Leah's tone grew serious.

Celadon City's Team Rocket base? That should be the one under the Game Corner, a significant base indeed.

Dylan mused.

"And guess who I saw, Dylan? Our old friend Archer is here too."

"Though I don't know what kind of base this is, I'm sure it's not simple. Archer seems to have brought something very valuable, locked in a safe."

"This Team Rocket base has at least three Executives!"

Leah lowered her voice, speaking to Dylan in a hushed tone.

A base with three Executives? Who knows what they're up to in there?

That's why Leah immediately thought of Dylan and Red—their big-league allies. She really hated Team Rocket!

The reason she didn't anonymously report the base was that Leah knew Celadon City's police force alone couldn't possibly take it down.

They might even alert the Team Rocket members and let them escape.

"You know, Dylan, you're my best friend. I'm telling you this first, so you can be a hero again!"

"This isn't a small matter, Leah. Be careful and don't act rashly." Dylan cautioned Leah.

"Hmm~ Don't worry. I've been wandering the world for so long; I know what I'm doing." Leah hummed.

"Alright, I'll be in Celadon City soon. We'll talk when we meet."

It was Saturday, a day Dylan had scheduled as a day off.

After hanging up, Dylan looked at Lance's contact in his phone, debating whether to call him.

Actually, after six days of growth simulation, Dylan's strength had greatly improved. Even with three Team Rocket Executives, he was confident in defeating them all.

But notifying Lance might be a good idea to arrange for Officer Jenny in Celadon City to assist.

"Is something wrong?" Ilene had overheard some of the conversation.

"Yeah, Leah discovered a large Team Rocket base, and I'm planning to go check it out." Dylan nodded.

At that moment, there was an @Everyone notification in the Gym Leader group chat.

Celadon Gym—Erika: @Everyone, oh my gosh! I discovered a Team Rocket base in Celadon City!

Celadon Gym—Erika: I'm about to infiltrate the base! For the League and Celadon City!!!

Officer Jenny: Team Rocket base??? Erika, calm down! Don't act alone—contact the local Officer Jennys and discuss it with them. Don't be impulsive.

Officer Jenny: @Erika, where are you? You didn't go in already, did you? Are you serious? (Frustrated.jpg)

Vermillion Gym—Surge: Whoa~ As expected of Erika! I, Surge, salute you! (Awesome.jpg)

Cerulean Gym—Daisy: Erika is the best!!

Fuchsia Gym—Koga: If I may be blunt, Erika is being reckless.

Pewter Gym—Brock: Though it's a bit rash, young people nowadays really are full of spirit. Cinnabar Gym—Blaine: She's a passionate kid, for sure.

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (Tilting Head in Confusion.jpg)

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: Indeed, courage is priceless. The future of our Pokémon League is bright~ (Gratified.jpg)

Rota Gym—Dylan: Wow

Officer Jenny: Aaaaah! Erika, you idiot! Do you think Team Rocket bases are that simple? She's going to drive me crazy!!!

Dylan looked at the group chat on his phone with a complex expression.

Leah had infiltrated the Team Rocket base. Why was Erika causing more trouble?

# 71: Chapter 71: Save Me!

Erika declared her plan to infiltrate the Team Rocket base right in front of the three Executives and the boss himself.

Dylan couldn't help but give her a thumbs-up. She truly had courage! Erika was indeed a brave Gym Leader!

"I'm sorry, Ilene, but I might not be able to go out with you today," Dylan apologized to Ilene.

They had planned to spend these days together, but with this unexpected situation, Dylan had to rush over.

The Team Rocket base in Celadon City was undoubtedly one of the largest in Kanto, and this conflict would likely escalate quickly.

Even if Giovanni now warned Archer and the others at the Celadon City base to retreat, it would be too late. There was too much important information in a large base to evacuate in such a short time.

Moreover, Officer Jenny was already in the group chat, and she would have already notified the League and the police in Celadon City.

"No problem, but please be careful."

Ilene was worried, but she still softly supported her childhood friend.

She wasn't a Trainer, so she couldn't go with Dylan. Otherwise, she would only hold him back, and Ilene didn't want to be a burden.

"Don't worry," Dylan replied with a smile.

He quickly sent a message to Lance, packed up his main Pokémon, stuffed a napping Clefairy into his backpack, and flew towards Celadon City on his Articuno.

---

Team Rocket Base in Celadon City

In the control room, Archer was reviewing some research data.

"How is it?" Proton asked, lounging on a chair.

"It failed again. The genetic rejection is severe," Archer shook his head. Then he picked up a ringing communication device and saw it was the boss calling.

"Boss, what? Yes, I understand."

Archer's already grim face darkened further as he respectfully ended the call.

Afterward, he looked at the surveillance screens, his expression shifting between uncertainty and concern.

"What's wrong?" Proton noticed something was off and dropped his relaxed demeanor.

"Ariana, come to the control room," Archer contacted another Executive.

"The boss said someone has already infiltrated the base."

"That's impossible," Proton frowned. This underground base was extremely concealed, built right beneath the Game Corner, with their people all over it.

"Nothing's impossible," Archer's face looked even more grim.

"Officer Jenny must have received the information as well. Back up the important data; we have to abandon this base," Archer said coldly.

The door to the control room opened, and a woman with red hair in a white Executive uniform walked in.

She was Ariana, another Executive and one of the four Generals of Team Rocket.

"Proton, you and Ariana find that intruder. We can't let her escape."

Archer continued working at the computer. The genetic fusion research and data they were conducting in this base were extremely important, and they had to be backed up quickly.

"Heh~ I'm quite good at catching rats."

A cruel smile slowly spread across Proton's face.

---

Celadon City

Located in the central Kanto region, it was one of the largest cities, second only to the metropolis of Saffron City.

The city's name, "Celadon," referred to the dreamlike colors of a rainbow, and it was also known as the Rainbow City.

Celadon City was famous in Kanto. Though not as developed as Saffron City, its department store was renowned throughout the region, and the Game Corner was the largest in Kanto. The top prize at the Game Corner was even a rare Dratini, making it famous far and wide.

Articuno's flying speed was quite fast, and Dylan arrived just outside Celadon City after about two hours.

He had Articuno land outside the city, recalled it, and then walked into Celadon City.

Normally, cities didn't allow Pokémon to carry people into them, mainly to avoid disrupting the cityscape and preventing accidental injuries.

Moreover, Articuno was one of Kanto's legendary Pokémon, far too conspicuous. Though Dylan didn't mind being the center of attention—he actually quite enjoyed it—the timing wasn't right.

Outside a hotpot restaurant near the Celadon Game Corner was the place where Dylan had arranged to meet Leah.

"There's been no movement for over two hours. Could she really have been caught?" Dylan checked the Gym Leaders' group chat and noticed Erika hadn't said anything in the chat.

If she really had infiltrated the Team Rocket base, not having time to check her phone was understandable. But there was also the possibility that she had already been caught.

After all, Giovanni would have certainly informed Archer that someone had infiltrated the base.

"Hey~"

Just then, a girl's voice came from behind Dylan.

"Why are you dressed like that?"

Dylan turned around to see a rather ordinary-looking black-haired girl wearing a T-shirt and a plaid skirt.

He gave Leah a strange look, especially at her chest, since the curve was shockingly large—easily a D-cup at least.

"Hey, hey, hey! What kind of look is that, you pervert!" The girl named Leah immediately crossed her arms and puffed up her cheeks, noticing Dylan's inappropriate gaze.

"It's not always about being bigger, you know. You might be dissatisfied with yourself, but we have to face reality," Dylan thought for a moment and sympathetically consoled Leah for her self-deception.

"What do you mean!! I've always been this big!" Leah was furious.

What do you mean by "facing reality"?!!

"Okay, okay," Dylan nodded in response.

"Dylan!!"

Leah gritted her teeth in frustration. She had only dressed this way for disguise, though she had a tiny bit of personal motive. But she was naturally a cute girl, so what's wrong with enhancing her appearance a bit?

"Did you call Red?"

"Yes, but it'll take him some time to get here," Leah turned away, awkwardly changing the subject.

"Alright, let's observe the surroundings first."

Dylan glanced toward the large, bustling Game Corner.

"Okay."

The two of them began walking around the Game Corner like ordinary passersby.

"With this information about the Team Rocket base, Officer Jenny must have already received the news. I also informed Champion Lance."

"You told Lance?" Leah was surprised.

"Yes, why?" Dylan was puzzled by her reaction.

"It's not a huge issue, but Lance's attitude towards Team Rocket is extreme," Leah whispered.

She had once secretly witnessed Lance wipe out a Team Rocket base.

"In the Kanto League, Lorelei and Lance are quite unique. Their approach to Team Rocket is extremely ruthless."

Leah explained in detail.

"Apparently, of the Team Rocket members captured by Lorelei, seven out of ten might suffer severe frostbite, leaving them with long-term injuries. If not treated in time, they might even freeze to death."

"And Lance is even more extreme. Only those who survive his attacks are eligible to be captured by the League!"

Leah enthusiastically described the scene, showing no sympathy for Team Rocket.

Lance's attack had left a deep impression on Leah. The Draco Meteor that rained down from the sky completely engulfed the entire Team Rocket base in continuous bombardment.

In that kind of attack, those hiding in the base were truly unlucky, and their survival depended entirely on luck.

Leah knew all too well what Team Rocket did in secret. Because of her childhood experiences, she had absolutely no sympathy for an evil organization like Team Rocket.

This was also one of the reasons she constantly caused trouble for them.

"I see," Dylan mused. It seemed that both Lance and Lorelei had extreme personalities.

As they chatted, the two soon arrived at a secluded alley behind the Game Corner.

"By the way, look there. That's where I snuck into the Team Rocket base."

Leah pointed to a hidden manhole cover in a corner.

"It was quite a coincidence. I felt something was off about the Game Corner and decided to investigate. Then I saw someone sneaking out of here, and when I went in, I discovered it led directly to the Team Rocket base."

"Impressive," Dylan praised Leah.

Just then, the manhole cover was pushed open, and a person in tight black clothing climbed out, coming face to face with Dylan and Leah.

It was a girl, as her curves were still noticeable under the tight clothing.

The girl hesitated for a moment, but upon seeing Dylan, her expression brightened with relief, and she limped toward him, nearly in tears.

"Dylan, save me!"

"My goodness! I don't understand it. I hadn't been inside for long, but it felt like the entire base was searching for me!!!"

(Chapter End)

# 72: Chapter 72: She Must Be Jealous

No, the fact that you managed to crawl out from there in one piece is already a miracle…

Dylan looked at the disheveled figure in black, or rather, Gym Leader Erika, and couldn't help but feel a bit exasperated.

That so-called Gym Leaders' group chat was more like a place for leaders to be critiqued. It also had another nickname, the Team Rocket Senior Executives' Spy Group.

Loudly plotting in front of the Team Rocket boss and three senior executives, isn't it normal to run into some trouble?

"Who are you?"

Despite his inner thoughts, Dylan appropriately showed a bit of caution, looking at the limping figure in black approaching.

"Erika! It's me, Erika, Gym Leader Dylan."

The figure in black hesitated, then quickly pulled down the black mask, revealing a pale but very delicate and beautiful face.

"Gym Leader Erika? You… be careful."

Dylan was about to say something when he saw a large purple cobra climbing out of the manhole behind Erika, its cold eyes fixed on her back.

Erika also felt the terrifying chill from behind, her body instantly stiffening.

Damn!

"Arti… Meowscarada."

Dylan's fingers expanded two minimized Poké Balls, intending to release Articuno, but remembering that Leah was with him, he instead released his Meowscarada.

Due to her childhood experiences, Leah had a severe fear of birds. Seeing a bird Pokémon would trigger her phobia.

"Meow~"

In a flash of white light, Meowscarada landed gracefully in front of Dylan, standing tall and haughtily gazing at the Arbok ahead.

The Arbok seemed to sense Meowscarada's hostility and pressure, hesitating with its tongue flicking.

"Gym Leader Erika, come here."

Seizing the opportunity, Dylan quickly pulled Erika behind him.

"Thank you, Gym Leader Dylan, I… ouch…"

Erika was extremely grateful, glad to have encountered Gym Leader Dylan!

But before she could finish, Erika gasped, biting her lip as cold sweat covered her pale face, unable to speak clearly due to the pain.

"She's poisoned."

Leah helped Erika sit down, and with her experience, she quickly lifted Erika's pant leg, having noticed earlier that Erika's leg seemed injured.

On inspection, she found four darkened, small puncture wounds, clearly from a snake bite.

"Yes, I'm sorry. I got bitten by an Arbok while escaping." Erika spoke weakly, enduring the pain.

"Gym Leader Erika, stop talking and use this."

Dylan took out a Full Heal from his backpack, one of the best medicines that could cure any abnormal status.

"Wow, such extravagance," Leah remarked, taking the antidote from Dylan and spraying it on Erika's wound.

Before their eyes, the dark purple around the wound began to recede noticeably, and soon the poison would be completely removed.

"So you have two companions, huh?"

A chilling female voice suddenly interrupted.

Dylan looked over to see a gloomy red-haired woman emerging from a back door, with two Team Rocket members behind her.

"Gnawing at wood, biting through bags, invading others' homes. Truly ill-mannered rats."

Ariana approached the Arbok, her sinister gaze fixed on Erika, mercilessly mocking her.

She was in a foul mood!

This base in Celadon was her responsibility, and now because of this intruder, they had to abandon it. How could she not be angry?

Erika's face changed, showing anger in her eyes, but she was too weak to respond.

"Is this a self-introduction, Team Rocket's Ariana?"

Leah gestured for Erika to rest and turned to Ariana with a taunting smile.

"If anyone resembles rats, it's you lot in Team Rocket, always hiding underground, away from the sun."

"Look at your complexion! You could star in a zombie movie without makeup~"

Leah spoke quickly but clearly.

The scene fell silent.

Erika looked at the seemingly ordinary girl in surprise. She… she was amazing!

The two Team Rocket members were shocked. How dare this girl insult Ariana, who had a terrible temper!

Ariana's face darkened, becoming almost twisted with rage. Was she being compared to a zombie?

"Hohoho… such a sharp-tongued little girl."

After a moment, Ariana laughed angrily, but her smile was terrifying.

"Once I tear that mouth off, I wonder if it will still be able to talk."

The malice in Ariana's eyes was chilling.

"Oh no, I'm so scared~"

Leah mockingly hugged herself, hiding behind Dylan, pretending to be frightened. Her exaggerated tone and performance made Ariana's face twitch with anger.

"Dylan, this zombie woman is so fierce. She wants to bully your cute little sister!"

"She must be jealous of my beauty, and because she can't have it, she wants to destroy me!"

Leah declared with a righteous fury.

Ariana was even more infuriated. Jealous? Of you? Have you seen your plain face? Why would I be jealous?

"How outrageous! Doesn't she know I'm Dylan's beloved? Too wicked! Right, Dylan?"

Leah clung to Dylan's sleeve, acting all delicate and vulnerable.

In other words, protect me!

Erika, the patient: Wow!

Dylan: "..."

Dylan looked speechlessly at Leah peeking from behind him, blinking her big eyes innocently.

Ariana's face was contorted with rage.

"Arbok!"

She couldn't take it anymore and wanted to capture this annoying girl!

At her command, the fierce Arbok opened its bloodthirsty mouth, revealing its fangs, and lunged at Dylan and Leah with alarming speed.

This was a Dark-type move, Crunch!

"Quick Attack." Dylan's brow furrowed.

A ghostly green shadow flashed by, and the Arbok, initially lunging, was suddenly hit as if struck by a truck, flying back at even greater speed, slamming into the wall heavily.

Ariana's pupils contracted.

"Meow~"

Meowscarada stood elegantly, slightly tilting its head, its sinister expression exuding a palpable malice.

It was Meowscarada who had knocked Arbok away.

"…"

Ariana looked at the Pokémon with an uncertain expression. This should be a Meowscarada from the Paldea region.

But as a senior executive of Team Rocket, Ariana was strong too! For her main Arbok to be knocked back without reacting…

Something wasn't right…

Ariana's anger began to cool. As one of Team Rocket's Four Generals, she wasn't foolish.

The Arbok slid down the wall, but its condition was visibly worse.

"What's wrong? Not attacking? I can give you another chance."

Seeing Ariana hesitate, Dylan spoke with his usual gentle tone, as if he were talking about something trivial.

"Don't be afraid. If you don't attack, I will. As a senior executive of Team Rocket, Ariana, handing you over to the League might earn me another pseudo-legendary Dragon-type Pokémon."

The gentlest voice, saying the most terrifying things.

"???"

Question marks floated above Ariana's head.

Ariana felt uneasy. Had she kicked a steel board?

(Chapter End)

# 73: Chapter 73: I Won't Let You Go

Ariana was very cautious. As a high-ranking officer of Team Rocket, she had always been a prime target for the Kanto League.

If she made a mistake, she could end up in jail, serving a life sentence. If she wasn't careful, she would have been captured long ago.

Moreover...

"You're the Gym Leader of the Rota Gym, Dylan?" Suddenly, Ariana seemed to recall something, frowning as she looked at Dylan.

"Is it an honor that Miss Ariana knows of me?" Dylan remained as polite as ever.

Seeing Dylan admit it, Ariana squinted slightly, her eyes flashing.

During the S.S. Anne incident, Team Rocket's Archer was defeated by two young boys, which caused quite a stir.

Ariana had asked Archer about it, and he said that if it hadn't been for a female clown interfering from the sidelines, he wouldn't have lost.

That female clown must have been that annoying girl.

So, judging by this, Dylan's strength should be less than Archer's? And she herself was only slightly weaker than Archer.

With this calculation, she should be able to take down Dylan? Ariana analyzed.

But the sudden attack just now felt a bit off, so she decided to test it out again.

Ariana made up her mind and threw out another Poké Ball.

A Vileplume, with an indigo body and a large flower on its head, appeared before Ariana.

"Dylan, what do you mean by getting a Pseudo-Legendary Dragon-type baby?"

Compared to Ariana's 'deep contemplation,' Leah was more concerned about what Dylan had said.

"After the S.S. Anne incident, the Kanto League rewarded me with a Dratini," Dylan admitted without hiding anything.

"Ah??" Erika was taken aback by this.

Even Leah, who had been calm, was also startled.

Dratini, the pre-evolution of Dragonite, was extremely rare and valuable. Given that it was a reward from the League, its talent was surely guaranteed.

A Dratini with excellent talent was so rare that it was virtually priceless on the black market.

"Such a loss! Such a huge loss!" Erika, realizing the significance, kept slapping her forehead in regret.

"But Dylan, can you handle her alone? After all, she is one of the Four Executives."

"What do you think?" Dylan chuckled at this quirky girl and turned his gaze back to Ariana.

Even a week ago, he was confident in defeating Ariana, let alone now.

His Meowscarada had already reached level 61!

"Miss Ariana, what do you think?"

"Arbok, use Fire Fang!"

Ariana did not respond verbally. Instead, with a serious expression, she ordered her Pokémon.

Arbok's fangs ignited with flames as it slithered towards Meowscarada.

"Still haven't learned your lesson." Dylan's tone was calm.

"Sucker Punch."

Meowscarada's gem-like eyes flashed with dark light, its claws surrounded by a black glow, and it disappeared in an instant.

A Dark-type Sucker Punch!

When it reappeared, Meowscarada was right in front of Arbok, giving the cobra a menacing grin before striking with its right claw.

Sucker Punch hit Arbok's neck hard, making it hiss in pain.

But Meowscarada didn't stop there. It lifted Arbok with its right claw and pushed it forcefully.

Bam!

Arbok was slammed against the wall, causing it to crack like a spider web.

Arbok closed its eyes in pain, its body going limp.

"Meow~!"

Meowscarada held Arbok high with one arm, tilting its head as it gazed at the fallen cobra. With a smug and sinister smile, it let out a mocking purr.

'Weren't you going to dance? Why can't you dance now?'

"Dylan, your Pokémon is quite wild." Leah watched in amazement. From any angle, this Meowscarada didn't seem like a tame Pokémon.

It had an unmistakable aura of wildness and evil!

Erika nodded in agreement with Leah, her eyes shining as she stared at Meowscarada. As a Gym Leader specializing in Grass-types, she was captivated by its wild and powerful charm.

Arbok, sensing Meowscarada's contempt, began to struggle again.

Boom!!

Meowscarada exerted force with its right arm, smashing Arbok's head into the wall, creating a deep crater.

"Arbok!"

Ariana's expression changed. This Meowscarada's strength was indeed unusual!

Even though Sucker Punch was a priority Dark-type move, it shouldn't be so fast that her Arbok couldn't react at all.

The most likely explanation was that this Meowscarada was much stronger than her Arbok!

"Vileplume, use Moonblast!"

Ariana immediately ordered Vileplume to attack. She had initially planned to use Arbok to distract Meowscarada and then have Vileplume ambush or attack Dylan and the others.

But unexpectedly, Arbok was defeated so quickly.

This was not the strength of an Elite Four member!

Ariana watched the sinister Meowscarada, gritting her teeth.

Although she was not as strong as Archer and the other top officers, she was second only to Archer among the Four Executives.

But now her main Pokémon, Arbok, was defeated in just a few moves. This was unreasonable!

"Sucker Punch." Dylan smiled.

Vileplume let out a cry as a virtual moon appeared behind it, scattering moonlight.

A Fairy-type Moonblast!

But before the attack could form, the ghostly Meowscarada appeared behind Vileplume, its sharp claws tearing through the air and striking down.

Sucker Punch hit directly, sending Vileplume flying and interrupting its Moonblast.

"Night Slash."

Meowscarada swiftly charged at Vileplume again, its face twisted in a wicked grin. Its purple-black claws slashed down at Vileplume.

A Dark-type Night Slash!

Night Slash struck Vileplume's chest hard, causing it to scream in agony.

Seeing Vileplume still howling, Meowscarada quickly struck again with Night Slash, sending Vileplume into a deep sleep.

Yes, Vileplume had fainted.

"This..."

Seeing Vileplume defeated so quickly, Ariana's face changed drastically. She was now certain!

This Meowscarada was probably Champion-level! Even if it was Elite Four-level, it shouldn't be able to crush her like a bug.

Archer, you bastard! Even if you didn't want to lose face in front of your colleagues, you shouldn't have lied about your defeat! This screwed her over!

If she had known Dylan was a Champion-level, she would have fled immediately! The difference between Elite Four and Champion-level was significant.

"Miss Ariana, it seems your strength isn't as impressive as I thought."

Dylan stepped forward, smiling faintly at the pale Ariana.

Meowscarada stood one step ahead of Dylan, arms crossed, tilting its head as it observed the three humans.

Ariana's face turned even paler, and the two Team Rocket members behind her were trembling.

Ariana gritted her teeth. There was only one option left.

"Mr. Dylan, it was my mistake. I apologize for my rudeness. Please forgive my earlier disrespect."

Ariana lowered her proud head, her tone softening as she pleaded with Dylan.

Very perceptive.

She had no choice; she couldn't win.

Ariana did have other Pokémon, but what was the point? This Meowscarada, like a ghost, had defeated her main Pokémon in a few moves.

And this Meowscarada didn't seem like a normal Pokémon at all!

Ariana feared that this dark, sinister Meowscarada might attack her next.

"Oh?" Dylan was surprised, his expression turning strange.

Erika and Leah were also surprised. Team Rocket's high-ranking officer was begging for mercy so quickly?

"Mr. Dylan, what do you think?"

Ariana glanced at Dylan's expression, hoping for a chance.

She had decided that if Dylan spared her, she would pack up and leave Celadon City as soon as she returned to the base!

Dylan pondered for a moment.

"Actually, even if you beg, I won't let you go."

Dylan quickly decided, speaking to Ariana.

"Ariana, you know the League will pay a good price for you. Maybe you could help me get a talented Larvitar or another Pseudo-Legendary Dragon-type."

Ariana's face froze.

"Anyway, Team Rocket is doomed. It would be good for you to get familiar with the prison environment early."

Dylan was serious; it was clear from his tone and expression.

End of Chapter

# 74: Chapter 74: Yey

"Dylan, don't go too far!"

Ariana was furious. This was simply an insult to her!

"Oh?" Dylan raised an eyebrow, and Meowscarada's eyes narrowed slightly.

"The Pokémon League is indeed stingy! Dylan, I'm willing to exchange myself for two Larvitars! Please forgive me this once."

Ariana bowed solemnly to Dylan.

Leah: "Pfft~"

Erika: (—)

"Are you serious?" Dylan smirked.

"Absolutely serious, Dylan! Though Team Rocket has a bad reputation, I, Ariana, always keep my word!"

Seeing a chance, Ariana became excited.

Simultaneously, she angrily kicked the two Team Rocket members behind her. These idiots had no situational awareness!

The two Team Rocket members were stunned, then they saw Ariana's cold and ruthless eyes, and shivered.

"Y-yes, that's right!"

"Lady Ariana might be harsh, but she has never lied to us."

The two Team Rocket members spoke with a hint of grievance.

"Lady Ariana, such authority! Even in this situation, you still have the mind to threaten your subordinates."

Leah spoke cheerfully, completely disregarding Ariana's dignity.

"Hahaha..."

Though ashamed and angry, Ariana forced a laugh and didn't retort.

You brat! If you weren't protected by someone strong, I would tear your mouth apart!

"Dylan, what do you think? If you let me go, I'll definitely deliver the two Larvitars!"

"Dylan, don't listen to her! How could someone as poor as her come up with two Larvitars? It's laughable."

Leah mocked mercilessly. Ariana herself used Pokémon like Arbok and Vileplume, yet she dared to claim she could produce two Larvitars.

"Miss! Our Team Rocket executives have long been rooted in the Kanto region. Do you think we can't get Larvitars?"

Ariana's face darkened as she sneered.

"Frog in a well! Whether it's Dratini or Larvitar, getting them is not hard for us Team Rocket!"

"Stealing from innocent trainers, right? You're so impressive." Leah's tone was sarcastic.

"If a trainer can't even protect their Pokémon, who can they blame?"

Ariana responded calmly, not seeing any fault in robbing others' Pokémon.

"Ridiculous! This is the classic 'blame the victim' mentality of a thief! Whether they are weak or not, it's no excuse for Team Rocket's crimes!"

Erika, regaining some strength, flushed with anger at Ariana's words.

She was a righteous and kind-hearted trainer, unable to accept Ariana's twisted logic.

"I'm too lazy to argue with you two. Dylan, please believe in my sincerity. You are a champion-level trainer; we wouldn't offend you for the sake of two Pseudo-Legendary infants."

Ariana coldly laughed twice, ignoring the two 'naive' girls, and looked respectfully at Dylan.

Two Pseudo-Legendary infants were indeed valuable, but it was worth trading them for a senior executive.

"Although Ariana's proposal is tempting, I'm afraid it's a no."

Dylan chuckled, signaling to Meowscarada.

Meowscarada, bursting with energy, was ready to pounce.

Ariana sensed danger and instinctively reached for the Poké Ball in her hand.

"Sorry, I'm a League Gym Leader."

As Dylan's words fell, a black-green shadow appeared behind Ariana in an instant.

"Meow~!"

A malicious whisper sounded in Ariana's ear, making her hair stand on end!

"Wait! Dylan, I..."

Ariana tried to speak, but a sharp pain in her neck made her black out and collapse.

Thud~ The unopened Poké Ball slipped from her hand to the ground.

The two Team Rocket members, sensing the situation, surrendered immediately, not wanting to face Meowscarada's wrath.

"Indeed, in a non-official battle, attacking the trainer directly is the most efficient."

Dylan picked up the Poké Ball, nodding in satisfaction.

"Meow~" Meowscarada appeared beside Dylan.

"Well done, Meowscarada. Without you, it wouldn't have been this easy." Dylan smiled warmly, praising Meowscarada, knowing its pride and attachment.

"Meow~!"

Meowscarada's eerie yet beautiful eyes narrowed into slits, expressing happiness.

"Erika, are you okay? You should rest for a bit."

Dylan looked at Erika, who was struggling to stand.

Though the Full Restore had cured her poisoning, complete recovery would take some time.

"I'm fine now. I already informed Officer Jenny, and she's on her way. Thank you so much, Dylan."

Erika continuously expressed gratitude. Without Dylan, she couldn't have escaped from Ariana in her poisoned state.

"That's good." Dylan glanced at the unconscious Ariana, planning to hand her over to Officer Jenny and later demand compensation from Lance.

"Erika, you were a bit reckless this time."

Dylan addressed Erika.

"In any case, you should have notified Officer Jenny or the League higher-ups first."

"Yes, I'm very sorry. I was too confident and didn't think it through." Erika blushed, sincerely apologizing.

"This infiltration has alerted them, and Team Rocket might withdraw soon."

Leah pondered. Erika's infiltration had been discovered, and Archer and the others were likely preparing to retreat.

"It's all my fault..." Erika felt very guilty.

"Don't worry. Officer Jenny has sealed off the area, and I informed Champion Lance. He should be arriving soon. None of these Team Rocket members will escape."

Dylan smiled reassuringly.

"Impressive, Dylan!"

Hearing this, Erika sighed in relief. If the Team Rocket members had escaped because of her, she would have felt terrible.

But she couldn't understand how the Team Rocket base's security was so good that she was discovered shortly after infiltrating...

Soon, Cerulean City's Officer Jenny arrived, taking the unconscious Ariana and the two miserable Team Rocket members away.

Then Dylan, Erika, and Leah headed to Erika's house, but...

Gym Leader Group Chat.

Celadon Gym—Erika: Phew~ I'm safe now. @Everyone, thank you for your concern. I made it out safely. (teary eyes.jpg)

Cerulean Gym—Daisy: Thank goodness~ Erika, it's great that you're okay!

Vermilion Gym—Lt. Surge: Hahaha~ Well done, Erika! (thumbs up.jpg)

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (tilted head, confused.jpg)

Celadon Gym—Erika: Lt. Surge, don't tease me. It's all thanks to Dylan. Without him, I might have been captured by Team Rocket. (puppy eyes.jpg)

Officer Jenny: I heard from Officer Jenny there. You should thank Dylan properly!

Celadon Gym—Erika: Sister, please don't scold me. I know I was wrong. (crying.jpg)

Vermilion Gym—Lt. Surge: Oh wow~ Did you capture Ariana? (surprised.jpg)

Dylan glanced at the excited Erika, who was typing away. Not bad.

Great, more fun!

# 75: Chapter 75: Rats?

Erika's Residence

"Please enjoy. My tea-making skills are not as refined as those of the masters, so please forgive me," Erika said with a gentle smile.

Erika had already bathed and changed into a beautiful kimono. She gracefully raised her fair, white wrist to pour tea for Dylan and Leah.

At this moment, Erika appeared elegant, serene, and gentle, a stark contrast to her lively demeanor in the group chat.

In fact, Erika was indeed a refined young lady who owned a perfume shop. She was also a highly skilled flower arranger. With her fair and pure appearance, she was the Gym Leader of Celadon City, making her the epitome of a wealthy, beautiful, and refined woman.

"Very nice," Dylan said with a smile after taking a sip.

"I'm really grateful to you, Dylan. If it weren't for you saving me, I don't know what might have happened," Erika said sincerely.

"Erika, you've mentioned this many times already," Dylan interrupted, shaking his head.

"As Gym Leaders of the Kanto League, it's only natural for us to help each other. Besides, you risked your life to investigate Team Rocket's base," Dylan said calmly.

"No matter what, it was still a lifesaving favor," Erika insisted.

Erika was very persistent. If Dylan hadn't saved her and she had been captured by Ariana and taken back to the Team Rocket base, Erika couldn't imagine what she would have faced.

She would probably have encountered many terrifying things since she was not only an infiltrator but also an official Gym Leader of the league, not a minor figure.

"That's right~ Erika, you should say, 'I owe you my life and can only repay you with my body,'" Leah teased, her cheeks puffed out like a hamster as she snacked and laughed mischievously.

"This..." Erika blushed deeply at Leah's words.

She was indeed very grateful to Dylan, but repaying him with her body was too much.

"Don't talk nonsense," Dylan said, glaring at Leah.

"What? Erika just wants to thank you, and she wouldn't be losing out," Leah muttered, rolling her eyes.

Leah thought Erika would be getting a great deal. Dylan had good looks, strength, wealth, and his talent as a trainer was monstrous!

Last week, he had fought alongside Red against Archer, and now he had captured Ariana!

"Shut up," Dylan said, stuffing a piece of cake into Leah's mouth.

Leah's eyes widened; the cake was too big!

"Don't mind Leah, she's just a bit mischievous," Dylan apologized to Erika.

"It's okay," Erika said, tucking a strand of hair behind her ear, avoiding eye contact with Dylan.

She knew Leah was joking, but as a reserved young lady, she found such comments a bit overwhelming. She wasn't as quirky as Leah.

"If you ever need anything, feel free to contact me. I won't refuse," Erika said with a serious tone and a modest smile.

"Alright, I won't be shy if I need your help," Dylan responded with a smile.

"By the way, Dylan, why is your Meowscarada so strong?" Leah asked curiously, taking another bite of cake.

"Meowscarada was my starter Pokémon. It's natural for it to be strong," Dylan said, shrugging innocently.

"Where did you get it, if I may ask?" Erika inquired, equally curious.

"I received it when I enrolled in a Pokémon school. I chose it as a Sprigatito because it felt like fate," Dylan explained, smiling.

In his Pokémon school, starter Pokémon were distributed, and sometimes, they included starters from other regions.

Like Liko in "Pokémon Horizons," who received a Sprigatito at the Pokémon school in Pallet Town.

"If you like Meowscarada, you could visit the Paldea region or ask a friend to send you one," Dylan suggested, sensing Erika's fondness for the Pokémon.

"Thank you, I understand," Erika said thoughtfully.

The three continued chatting over tea. Meanwhile, the Celadon City police had begun their operation.

The area around the Game Corner was temporarily cordoned off by Officer Jenny and her team, evacuating guests and residents.

Team Rocket was the largest criminal organization in the Kanto region. Once discovered, they would not be tolerated!

However, the Game Corner was a popular spot, so evacuating everyone took time.

With a roar, a fierce Aerodactyl descended into Erika's courtyard.

A young man with fiery red hair leapt off the Aerodactyl. It was the Champion, Lance.

"Lance, you arrived quickly," Dylan remarked as the three of them stepped out of the house.

Disguised, Leah curiously observed the Kanto Champion, who exuded a dragon-like arrogance and imposing presence. Erika, on the other hand, was a bit cautious. She had heard many rumors about Lance, all suggesting he was arrogant and difficult to get along with.

"I heard from Officer Jenny. You did well, Dylan," Lance said with a rare smile, looking at Dylan with approval.

No wonder he was Lance's junior!

Although Lance despised Ariana, he had to admit that Team Rocket's Executives were cunning, making them difficult to capture.

But Dylan had managed to capture Ariana, making Lance increasingly fond of his junior. As expected, Viridian Forest chose wisely!

"When I return to Indigo Plateau, I will personally request a reward for you from the chairman. What do you want?" Lance asked generously.

"Thank you, Champion Lance. I would like a gifted Pokémon," Dylan said after a moment of thought. "If possible, a Larvitar or another pseudo-legendary would be great."

Lance raised an eyebrow, finding his junior quite ambitious.

"Having ambition is necessary," Lance said with a grin.

A well-trained pseudo-legendary could become a team's backbone or even a star player!

"But Dylan, raising dragons takes time and effort. How's the Dratini I entrusted to you?" Lance asked sternly. He hoped Dylan wasn't neglecting his Pokémon in pursuit of stronger ones.

"Don't worry, Champion Lance. I won't neglect any of my Pokémon," Dylan assured.

"That's good to hear," Lance said, nodding in satisfaction.

"I will discuss your request with the president. I can't guarantee approval, but it shouldn't be a problem," Lance said calmly.

Ariana was one of Team Rocket's Executives and a formidable trainer. Capturing her justified the request for a pseudo-legendary.

"If the chairman disagrees, I'll personally give you another Dratini with a different ability," Lance added, remembering Dylan's Dratini had the Shed Skin ability.

Hearing Lance's casual tone, Leah and Erika were stunned.

Is Dratini really that worthless?

"Thank you in advance, Champion Lance," Dylan said, accepting the offer. Though he already had a Dragonite, having more was never a bad thing, especially as a gesture from Lance.

"You and I both received gifts from Viridian Forest. You can call me Senior," Lance said softly.

"Okay, Senior Lance!" Dylan responded, sensing Lance's different attitude towards him. Lance had been focused on Dylan the entire time, not sparing a glance at Leah or Erika.

Clearly, in Lance's eyes, they weren't worth his time.

"Let's go take care of those filthy rats," Lance said, leaping back onto Aerodactyl with a flick of his cape.

Team Rocket must be eliminated!

Lance's gaze was icy.

By now, it was half-past six in the evening, and the sky had darkened.

Aerodactyl hovered mid-air, with Lance standing tall, his red hair blowing in the night wind as he gazed expressionlessly at the massive Game Corner below.

On the ground, officers stood ready.

Outside the area, a middle-aged man in a suit and black bowler hat squinted up at Lance.

"Boss?"

"Not yet. Wait."

The man's magnetic voice was soft. If Proton and Apollo could escape with important data, there would be no need for him to intervene.

(End of Chapter)

# 76: Chapter 76: He's a Criminal!

"Champion Lance, there are no signs of any Team Rocket members yet."

A leading Officer Jenny reported through her radio to Lance. She had been discreetly surrounding the game corner with her team since the afternoon.

Except for a few Team Rocket members, they hadn't seen anyone else.

It was likely because of Ariana's 'disappearance' that the Team Rocket members in this underground base had become more cautious.

"Rats will always be rats. Have we confirmed that there are no other exits around this base?" Lance asked coldly.

"Yes! We have thoroughly checked and are guarding the surrounding area."

"Then let's begin."

Lance replied calmly. If it were his usual style, he would have his two Dragonites unleash a dual Draco Meteor on the game corner.

But this was a large underground Team Rocket base, and there could be many innocent Pokémon trapped inside.

Despite his hatred for Team Rocket and his extreme stance towards them, Lance's love for Pokémon was genuine.

Following Lance's orders, Lorelei led several Officer Jennys and elite officers into the game corner.

At the back of the game corner, two Officer Jennys, led by Arcanine, cleared the way, with Dylan and Leah following behind.

Arcanine's keen sense of smell could detect Team Rocket's ambushes and locate their positions promptly.

"Ariana previously exited through the back door. There must be a secret passage nearby that leads into the base."

Inside the game corner, Leah analyzed. In the afternoon, there were still tourists in the game corner, and Ariana wouldn't have walked around inside with two Team Rocket members.

The two Arcanines quickly identified something amiss, leading them to a tile. Suddenly, a burst of Flamethrower erupted.

With the explosion of Flamethrower, the tile blew up, revealing a tunnel.

"Gym Leader Dylan, according to the plan, shall we split up?"

Entering the underground base, an Officer Jenny asked Dylan.

"Sure, but make sure to prioritize your safety."

Dylan nodded, reminding the officers.

"Don't worry!" Officer Jenny nodded seriously. They were well-trained officers.

"I'll also go solo, Leah. We'll contact each other once we find the lab."

Leah observed her surroundings, waved to Dylan, and headed off.

Their main goal was to find the laboratory and control room in this underground base, prioritizing the rescue of trapped Pokémon.

After the Officer Jennys and Leah left, Dylan opened two Poké Balls. Salazzle appeared in front of him, and beside Salazzle was a bipedal, dark purple Pokémon.

It resembled a bipedal monster from a tokusatsu show, with skin as hard as stone, a thick tail, and a white chest and belly.

This was a Nidoking, evolved from Nidorino!

'Nidoking, Poison/Ground type, Drill Pokémon.

Ability: Sheer Force

Moves: Megahorn, Earth Power, Poison Jab, Toxic, Toxic Spikes, Sucker Punch (Egg Move), Earthquake, Thunderbolt…

Description: Its thick tail packs enormous destructive power, capable of breaking steel towers. Once it goes into a rage, there's no stopping it.'

This Nidoking was one of Dylan's main Poison-type Pokémon, and it was very strong.

"The space here is a bit tight, but it doesn't matter."

Dylan smirked, noticing four Team Rocket members in black uniforms quickly approaching.

Undoubtedly, they had detected the disturbance and were coming to investigate.

"Quick! Notify General Archer, we've found an intruder!"

"The others have left; there's only one here."

"Just one person? How arrogant! Take him down!"

"League lapdog! You dare walk into a trap!"

The four Team Rocket members shouted, then threw their Poké Balls.

Golbat, Arbok, Gloom, and Machoke glared menacingly at Nidoking and Salazzle.

"Is it just you?"

Dylan asked.

"???"

The four Team Rocket members were stunned.

"So, it's just you four. Can you tell me where Archer is?" Dylan asked again.

Compared to these ordinary Team Rocket members, he was more interested in finding Archer.

"Stop joking! Who would tell you where General Archer is?"

The four Team Rocket members became wary.

"It seems you won't cooperate. But that's fine, you'll talk when it hurts."

Dylan chuckled, stepping back and signaling to Nidoking and Salazzle.

Nidoking growled, golden electricity crackling on its body.

Salazzle's mouth filled with a red, searing flame.

"You arrogant jerk! There are four of us!"

The large Team Rocket member leading the group was furious. Even if he looked down on them, there had to be a limit.

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Golbat, Wing Attack!"

"Machoke, use Brick Break!"

"Gloom, use Razor Leaf!"

"Arbok, use Ice Fang!"

The four Pokémon attacked together, and then… there was nothing.

Nidoking's Thunderbolt and Salazzle's Flamethrower swiftly crushed the four Pokémon. The powerful attack sent the four Team Rocket members flying, crashing to the ground.

One unfortunate member hit his face, causing a bloody wound, and he gasped in pain.

The other three were also injured, looking miserable.

"Pathetic. You're even weaker than I expected."

Dylan slowly walked to the four Team Rocket members, noting their terrified expressions.

Nidoking and Salazzle stood like bodyguards beside him.

This wasn't a Gym; he had to be cautious and ensure his safety.

The four Team Rocket members blushed with shame, but the pain reminded them that this seemingly harmless young man was terrifying!

"Good, Team Rocket pays you to do a job, so don't throw your lives away for them. It's not worth it."

"Even as a worker, you must think of yourself."

Dylan was pleased with their fearful response.

The four nodded like chickens pecking at grain.

"So, tell me, where is Archer?"

Dylan smiled charmingly, leaning forward.

The four hesitated; would they betray their leader?

"Hmm?" Dylan narrowed his eyes.

Nidoking stepped forward, its fierce gaze sweeping over them, crackling with electricity.

The oppressive aura from Nidoking made the four shiver, turning pale.

"General Archer is still in the control room!"

"Yes! He was copying data earlier, so he hasn't left."

"Archer? He's an unforgivable criminal!"

"By the way, sir, Archer brought a special item to the base. We can show you the way!"

They spoke quickly.

"Good, good, you're very sensible," Dylan praised them.

"Team Rocket has no future; the League is the standard. I'm glad you're turning over a new leaf."

"And you mentioned a special item?"

Dylan looked at the member with the face wound.

If he remembered correctly, Leah had also mentioned that Archer brought something important to this base.

"Yes, sir, I greeted Archer when he arrived. I'm sure it was a Poké Ball!"

The Team Rocket member affirmed.

"A Poké Ball?"

Dylan pondered. What kind of Poké Ball would Archer value so much? The Master Ball hadn't been developed yet.

Or was it about the Pokémon inside the Poké Ball?
