# 77: Chapter 77: (Serious Face)

"Interesting."

Dylan's lips curled up slightly. What Archer valued certainly wasn't something ordinary.

Boom~!

A faint explosion echoed from afar. Dylan glanced in that direction, clearly indicating that the main force had already stormed the base and was fighting Team Rocket.

"You guys, leave through the back door. Go surrender and if you're sincere, you probably won't be detained for long."

Having obtained the location of the room from these Team Rocket members, Dylan spoke to the four.

"Don't do bad things anymore. Not every League trainer is as easygoing as I am."

Dylan reminded them, heading in that direction.

He had given the warning and the opportunity. If these four Team Rocket members still insisted on throwing their lives away, it wasn't his fault.

"Thank you, sir!"

"Quick, quick! Let's go."

The four got up, enduring their injuries, and hurriedly left the Team Rocket base, although they still had Pokémon capable of fighting.

But they weren't foolish. With the Pokémon League already storming in, staying there was a death sentence.

"Are we really going to surrender?"

"Are you stupid? Surrendering now means a lighter sentence. This base is doomed. When we get out of jail, we can do whatever we want."

"I've been wanting to leave this lousy Team Rocket for ages. Can't even see the sun all day. My hard-earned tan has faded. Damn it!"

"Let's go, hurry, before the League gets here."

The four helped each other, eagerly heading to surrender to Officer Jenny for the first time in their lives.

Meanwhile, Dylan, following the directions given by the four, turned a corner.

"League lapdog! Your journey ends here!"

A male and female Team Rocket member shouted righteously.

Dylan looked at the female Team Rocket member with interest. Team Rocket's black uniform was quite stylish, showing the designer had a decent sense of aesthetics.

Unlike the bizarre uniforms of later villainous organizations.

"Nidoking."

After a quick glance, Dylan called out to Nidoking.

Nidoking bared its teeth and stepped forward.

And then...

"Damn! How can they be so strong!"

The male Team Rocket member fell to his knees, pounding the ground in frustration.

"Don't come any closer! I don't want to be turned into a puff by Nidoking!"

The female Team Rocket member, pale and trembling, stared in terror at the approaching Nidoking.

"???"

Dylan, walking behind Nidoking, gave the fairly pretty and well-built female Team Rocket member a peculiar look.

What kind of inappropriate stuff does this woman watch?

"You're too weak. Get a proper job. Why join an evil organization?"

Dylan shook his head, continuing with Nidoking and Salazzle.

Along the way, they encountered several more Team Rocket members. However, all were easily defeated by Nidoking and Salazzle.

Most of these Team Rocket members were sensible enough to step aside after losing, letting Dylan pass.

No one wanted to test the power of a Pokémon on their own body.

Although Team Rocket was evil, they weren't stupid. Fighting Pokémon after being defeated would be suicidal.

Some were even accidentally injured by the battle's aftermath, and Dylan could only say they were unlucky.

A famous philosopher once said, "To step over an ant without killing it is a difficult power to master."

Similarly, defeating them while ensuring their safety was also challenging.

"This guy!"

In the control room, a Team Rocket member watched the surveillance footage of the rampaging dark-clad youth, his face red with anger.

This was their base, yet this guy strolled around like it was nothing. Didn't Team Rocket care about their reputation?

And he was mocking them!

Seeing the youth smile at the camera and flash a peace sign made the Team Rocket member grit his teeth in anger.

Are you clocking in? Damn it!!!

"Looks like this is it."

Dylan didn't even glance at the limping Team Rocket members fleeing. He focused on the small, locked room ahead.

The steel door looked sturdy. Dylan took a step back, examining it.

"Nidoking, open the door."

Nidoking roared, lowered its head, and rammed into the door.

With a loud crash, the steel door shattered. Nidoking clawed at the remaining pieces, clearing the way.

"Good job."

Dylan praised Nidoking, entering the room.

The room was small, with a black safe in the corner being the most eye-catching item.

"Nidoking." Dylan hesitated but then asked Nidoking to act again. He didn't know the password.

Nidoking's claws glowed with a dark aura, using a Dark-type move, Hell Thrust.

With a few slashes, the safe broke open.

"It's really a Poké Ball." Dylan glanced inside, seeing only an ordinary red and white Poké Ball.

"What kind of Pokémon is it?"

Dylan curiously took the Poké Ball, turning it around. It looked unremarkable.

But since Archer valued it so much, it had to be extraordinary.

"Come out."

Dylan decided to open the Poké Ball.

A flash of white light revealed a black Pokémon before him.

Seeing this Pokémon, Dylan was shocked. Its appearance was unforgettable, instantly recognizable.

No way, how could this Pokémon be here?

Thoughts raced through Dylan's mind. It didn't make sense. Team Rocket, though the underworld ruler of Kanto, hadn't extended their reach to other regions yet.

Wait...

Dylan suddenly remembered the man he saw in the Vermilion City hotel, and everything became clear.

No way?

Thinking of that possibility, Dylan was stunned. Was that guy so weak that he lost his Pokémon?

The black Pokémon stood silently, head down, its aura weak and spirit low.

"Oh my, young man, sneaking into someone else's base and taking their stuff is quite rude, you know?"

A lazy voice suddenly came from the doorway, carrying a chilling undertone.

Dylan looked up. A green-haired, handsome young man stood there, one of Team Rocket's high-ranking officers, Proton, whom Dylan had met on the S.S. Anne.

The black Pokémon also looked at Proton, blue eyes filled with complex emotions. It was him!

"Young man, that belongs to Team Rocket. Could you return it to me?" Proton smiled, extending his hand to Dylan.

"Oh?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow, pocketing the Poké Ball. Adjusting his collar, he slowly spoke.

"What do you mean, Team Rocket's? This is clearly my Pokémon! If you keep talking nonsense, I won't be polite! (Serious face)"

"???" Proton's expression froze.

Are you kidding me? Am I the one talking nonsense? Can you lie any more blatantly?

Are you in Team Rocket or am I?

(End of the chapter)

---

# 78: Chapter 78: Urgent Rescue Needed

Proton was a bit taken aback. His memory wasn't bad, and he definitely remembered seeing this dark-clad youth on the S.S. Anne.

Upon investigation, it turned out this person was indeed a trainer from Rota Gym and the Gym Leader too.

But the words he was saying...

Since when did Gym Leaders of the Pokémon League become so shameless?

"Young man~ that joke isn't very funny."

Proton's face twitched slightly as he tried to maintain his usual facade of a smiling tiger.

"As a Gym Leader of the Pokémon League, stealing someone else's Pokémon is quite unethical."

Proton's tone was laced with undisguised sarcasm.

"Tsk~ Proton, do you have any evidence?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"You say I stole your Pokémon, but do you have any proof?"

"???"

Proton was amused. Did he really need proof? Look at the destroyed door and the broken safe.

Calling you a robber wouldn't be an exaggeration.

Legitimate League members storming a Team Rocket base would be arresting members left and right.

But you? You just came in and started looting, didn't you?

"Young man, isn't Darkrai right beside you? And you took its Poké Ball too."

Yes, this black Pokémon was indeed Darkrai!

"That's right." Dylan nodded nonchalantly.

"But do you have any proof that Darkrai belongs to Team Rocket? Why don't you call it and see if it responds to you?"

Dylan glanced at Darkrai, which was also looking at him.

This Darkrai seemed off, not only physically weak but also mentally exhausted. Who knows what it had gone through?

"..." Proton's eyes darkened.

There was no way Darkrai would respond to him. It was a blessing that Darkrai hadn't attacked him.

"Young man, you..."

"What do you mean, you? I have a question for you, Proton."

Ignoring Proton's dark look, Dylan took a step forward, speaking loudly and righteously, cutting off Proton's words.

"What is Team Rocket's deal? Locking my Darkrai in your safe?!"

Proton was stunned into silence as Dylan's voice boomed.

"If you don't give me a satisfactory explanation today, Proton, you'll be spending some time in jail!"

Sensing their trainer needed support, Nidoking and Salazzle stepped forward. Nidoking wore a fierce expression, and Salazzle sneered. The atmosphere changed instantly!

Full Villain Mode.jpg

"You!"

Proton was shocked!

Good heavens! This was the first time he had seen a Gym Leader from the Pokémon League act like this!

You're falsely accusing me with such audacity, and you don't even blush! Who taught you this?!

"Fine, fine, fine!!!"

Finally regaining his composure, Proton's sinister face twisted as he pulled down his hat brim.

"It seems you won't be returning Darkrai to us, so be it. I find you quite interesting as well."

Proton's voice turned cold. He wasn't a saint.

Two Poké Balls opened, revealing Crobat and Weezing.

"Tsk~ just right, your bounty in the League is quite high too." Dylan smiled brightly.

Capturing him and handing him over to the League would surely enhance Dylan's performance record, resulting in richer rewards.

"Heh~" Proton sneered.

This kid really had no idea of the danger he was in. Just because he defeated Archer with help, he thought he could handle Proton too?

Such laughable arrogance!

Darkrai remained silent, seemingly invisible, but as the two were about to battle, its blue eyes flickered, focusing on Crobat.

This Crobat wasn't unfamiliar. Darkrai's eyes grew more complicated.

"Crobat, Air Slash!"

"Weezing, Psybeam!"

Proton stepped back to a safe distance, holding a Poké Ball ready to throw, and quickly commanded his two Pokémon.

This wasn't a standard battle, and Proton, as an experienced trainer, knew it well.

He couldn't be sure of this youth's street-fighting experience, but from his shameless words, he could guess he was a rogue.

"Feeble attack."

Dylan glanced at them and clicked his tongue.

"Nidoking, Thunderbolt."

Nidoking roared, its burly body crackling with electricity. Golden lightning shot towards the Air Slash and Psybeam.

Electric-type Thunderbolt!

Boom!

The three attacks clashed violently, but in the next moment, Thunderbolt broke through Air Slash and Psybeam, shooting towards Crobat and Weezing.

Crobat dodged quickly, flying up, while Weezing was hit directly, crying out in pain.

"This power? Something's wrong."

Proton kept retreating, shielding his face from the blast. His expression soured as he struggled to keep his eyes open.

But the key issue was how this Nidoking's Thunderbolt could be so strong?

Sheer Force ability?

Sheer Force removes the additional effects of moves but boosts their power by 30%. It's a powerful ability for battle.

As the smoke cleared, Proton noticed something was wrong. A long, dark shadow leapt out, flames building in its mouth.

"Crobat! Watch out!"

Proton quickly warned Crobat.

A fiery pillar surged towards the airborne Crobat, slamming it against the wall.

Fire-type Flamethrower!

Boom!

The flames erupted, leaving Crobat covered in embers, nearly knocked out by the Flamethrower.

Salazzle landed gracefully, sneering at Crobat with mocking eyes.

"..."

Proton's face darkened, his eyes flickering.

He didn't expect his Pokémon to be hit so quickly.

The atmosphere grew tense.

Tap, tap~

Footsteps echoed as Dylan emerged from the small room, standing in the wider hallway, staring at Proton.

Nidoking kept its massive body between Dylan and any threat.

Darkrai hesitated but slowly floated after Dylan.

"Mr. Proton, it seems your strength is lacking." Dylan smirked.

"Heh~ Don't get cocky, young man. Let's see who laughs last."

Proton was enraged but sneered. This guy dared to mock him during battle? How did such an uncivilized person become a Gym Leader?

What were the League's inspectors doing?!

"Such a hard mouth, but Mouthpiece isn't here." Dylan sighed, missing little Leah's sharp tongue.

If Leah were here, she would be spitting verbal bullets by now.

"Something's off about this guy's strength."

Compared to Dylan, Proton felt immense pressure, discreetly pulling out a communicator.

Dylan noticed Proton's subtle move but couldn't stop him. No problem. Unless Giovanni himself came, no one could save Proton today.

A buy one, get one deal would be even better.

And if Giovanni really risked exposure to come, it wouldn't matter. The Articuno above wasn't for nothing.

It would be a perfect chance to see Lance and Giovanni clash! Dylan had even prepared a camera for this possibility.

Meanwhile, Proton urgently sent a distress signal to Archer.

'Danger! Urgent rescue needed! .jpg'

(End of the chapter)

---

# 79: Chapter 79: Who Told You to Bring Dylan Here!

In the control room, Archer was still transferring data.

A glance at the monitors showed that battles between Team Rocket and League trainers were taking place throughout the base.

Archer's expression was serious; he needed to hurry. Ariana was missing, and only he and Proton were left as high-level combatants in the base.

This genetic fusion research data was extremely important. The base was definitely lost, but the data had to be taken away. Otherwise, the loss to Team Rocket would be too severe.

"Huh? The lab has also been breached?"

Archer looked towards the lab and saw an ordinary-looking girl with a Wartortle breaking in. What a pity.

Although the genetic fusion research wasn't perfect, they had indeed created a semi-finished product. Speaking of which...

"What is Proton doing?"

Archer frowned. He had sent Proton to retrieve Darkrai, but he still hadn't returned.

Even though this League operation was led by Lance, he hadn't made a move yet, right? Darkrai, as a legendary mythical Pokémon, was indeed precious. Even though the boss didn't value it highly and its gene fusion with Mewtwo had failed.

But if a Team Rocket executive could capture it and train it well, its strength would surely increase significantly.

Just then, Archer's communicator suddenly lit up.

"?"

Archer picked up the communicator and saw that it was a distress call from Proton?

What was going on with Proton? The Kanto League hadn't dispatched any other Elite Four members, had they? Had he really fallen to the point where he couldn't even handle ordinary League trainers?

Archer glanced at the data transfer, which was nearly complete, and hesitated for a second.

Can't save him; you'll have to fend for yourself, brother!

Archer felt a bit sorry but clearly prioritized the important research data over Proton.

Five minutes later, Archer looked at the successful data transfer with satisfaction.

He immediately put the USB drive into his inner pocket and deleted the entire system. Now he just needed to retreat.

Although the base was certainly surrounded by League members led by Lance, it didn't matter! A cunning rabbit has three burrows. This large base had a very hidden exit, known only to high-level executives.

Archer turned to leave, but just then...

Boom!

The control room door was blasted open with a loud explosion, and the deafening blast and smoke made Archer cough uncontrollably, caught off guard.

What was happening? The enemy had reached this far???

Archer's face was extremely grim.

A shaky figure staggered in through the blasted 'door.'

"Archer!!"

"Proton?!"

Archer couldn't believe his eyes as he saw the tattered, disheveled figure. Despite the dust-covered face, missing hat, and ragged clothes, it was undeniably his colleague, Proton.

How did he end up like this in just ten minutes? Did he go through some survival challenge in the wilderness?! Proton's eyes lit up when he saw Archer, running towards him as if he had found a savior.

"Great! You're still here, Archer!" Proton was nearly in tears.

"What happened to you?" Archer's eyelid twitched. His intuition told him something was very wrong.

"That guy's a monster! Can't win! Absolutely can't win!"

"What are you talking about?"

"Tsk~ Fate brings us together again, Mr. Archer. I didn't expect us to meet so soon."

Suddenly, a clear voice interrupted.

Archer was stunned, and Proton immediately hid behind him.

"Dylan?"

Seeing the young man at the 'door' with a Nidoking and Salazzle, and a weakened Darkrai behind him, Archer frowned.

Was Dylan the one who beat Proton like this? Archer was a bit shocked. The incident on the S.S. Anne was still fresh in his mind; it hadn't been long.

But something was off. Dylan was strong, but Proton shouldn't be completely outmatched by him.

"Ah~ It's an honor to be remembered by Mr. Archer."

Dylan, looking like an elegant and gentle gentleman, had a bright gaze fixed on Archer.

Two of the Four Executives, plus Ariana from before—three high-ranking Team Rocket executives. The Kanto League's rewards should be quite significant.

Maybe I should apply for a Shiny Beldum? Mega Metagross is pretty cool, though there's no Beldum in the Kanto region.

But with such a big achievement, couldn't President just ask the Hoenn League for one? "I guessed that Mr. Archer hadn't left yet. I didn't expect Proton to actually lead the way."

Dylan's smile grew warmer.

So you led the enemy here? I could have retreated safely, you idiot! Archer shot a fierce glare at Proton, who remained defiant.

"Archer, I underestimated you before. I didn't expect you to handle even this guy. Impressive!"

Proton gave Archer a thumbs-up. He had always thought Archer, as the leader of the Four Executives, was great at strategy but not much stronger than the rest.

But now, it seemed Archer had been hiding his true strength all along! Even someone as strong as Dylan needed a 2v1 to beat Archer. This showed how valuable Archer was as the leader of the Four Executives!

"Huh?" Archer was momentarily confused by Proton's comment.

But that wasn't the point. The point was Dylan.

"Dylan, it truly is a coincidence. How about my previous offer?"

Archer calmed down, stood tall with his left hand behind his back, looking elegant. Previous offer? Oh, he wanted to recruit me into Team Rocket.

"In this situation, Mr. Archer, are you joking again?" Dylan's smile widened.

"To be honest, Ms. Ariana is already in custody, getting familiar with the environment. I imagine she must be quite lonely. You wouldn't want to leave your colleague all alone, would you?"

So Ariana was captured by Dylan?

Archer and Proton were taken aback but quickly understood.

"I see. Dylan, how about we each take a step back? Consider Darkrai a gift from Team Rocket to you."

Archer's eyes flickered, then he decisively spoke to Dylan.

Now wasn't the time to fight Dylan. Lance, the Kanto Champion, was still out there, watching. The longer they stayed, the greater the risk.

And Dylan capturing Ariana and defeating Proton like this didn't add up.

Did Dylan hide his true strength on the S.S. Anne?

Archer, being highly intelligent, considered this possibility and didn't naively trust the previous battle.

"Mr. Archer still loves to joke." Dylan shrugged and stepped back two steps, casually waving his hand.

Nidoking and Salazzle understood Dylan's intent, stepping forward with fierce glares at Archer and Proton, creating an oppressive atmosphere.

"So, we can't come to an agreement?"

Archer's dark face grew serious as he released Houndoom and Weezing from their Poké Balls.

"Of course, Mr. Archer. As friends, I'll visit you often." Dylan's tone was gentle, as if talking to an old friend.

Archer's face darkened. Visit me in prison, huh?

"Dylan! You don't understand. Last time, you teamed up with Red, and there was a clown interfering. That's why I lost!"

Archer spoke in a low voice, but this time he was in peak condition! "Go for it, brother!! Take down this uncivilized guy!" Proton cheered on Archer like a cheerleader.

"Dylan! Bring it on!"

I'll win!!!

And then...

"How can he be so strong?"

Archer clutched his chest, shocked at the sight of his defeated Houndoom and Weezing.

How did this happen? The battle hadn't lasted long, yet his main Pokémon were already down!

Finally coming to his senses, Archer glared at the equally stunned Proton.

"You actually led such a strong enemy to me? Are you insane?"

I could have retreated safely! It's all because of this idiot Proton. He knew Dylan was so strong, yet he brought him here!

Is Proton brainless? Does he have to drag someone down with him?!

Now what? Dylan was blocking the door; how could they escape?! "Hey, don't blame me! Didn't you say it took both Dylan and Red to beat you? I thought you could handle it!"

Proton retorted, feeling wronged. How was he supposed to know Archer was bluffing?

(End of the chapter)

# 80: Chapter 80: Why Not Be a Decent Person?

"You!"

Archer was both amused and angered by Proton. So now he's being accused of lying? But he wasn't lying!!

Back on the S.S. Anne, it was indeed Dylan and Red who teamed up 2v1 against him, with that Harley Quinn-like girl constantly using 'Mental Pollution,' which is why he lost.

Dylan watched with great interest as Archer and Proton started to bicker. This was too entertaining.

"We're all brothers here, let's not argue—it'll hurt our camaraderie. How about you two just have a fight instead?" Dylan tried to play the peacemaker.

"??"

Both Archer and Proton looked at Dylan with confusion, their faces turning grim.

"It seems you're not willing. So, are you still going to fight me? If so, hurry up and bring out your Pokémon," Dylan said, a bit disappointed. Seeing the way Archer and Proton were acting, it seemed they wouldn't fight after all.

But then again, they were high-ranking executives in Team Rocket.

Archer took a deep breath, calming his agitated emotions.

"Dylan, I think we can talk this out," Archer said, trying to keep his voice steady.

No, fighting was out of the question. Dylan was too strong.

Looking at Proton, it seemed his main Pokémon had already been taken down by Dylan. Although Archer still had some of his main Pokémon, what was the use?

They had no choice but to negotiate. Archer didn't want to end up in jail; he still had work to do for Team Rocket!

"At this point, there's nothing left to discuss, Mr. Archer," Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"As a League employee, I've already shown mercy by not attacking you and Mr. Proton directly."

"Lance is still watching from above, Mr. Archer. Why not be a decent person?" Dylan shook his head slowly. He believed he had already gone easy on them. If it had been Lorelei or Lance, whether Archer and Proton could still be standing would be a big question mark.

"Dylan, I don't think Team Rocket has wronged you. Ariana has already been arrested, and that achievement is enough."

Archer didn't give up but spoke sincerely to Dylan.

"If you turn a blind eye and let Proton and I leave, or even just one of us, Team Rocket will reward you far more generously than the League."

He still had critically important genetic fusion data, the product of years of research at this base. He had to get it out!

"I'm afraid I have to refuse. I'm a Gym Leader. Please excuse the both of you," Dylan smiled, directly refusing.

What a joke—though he did enjoy watching entertaining and thrilling scenes, what benefit would there be in letting Archer and Proton go? Even if there were benefits, they wouldn't be worth it.

"Salazzle."

Salazzle bared its teeth and began to approach Archer and Proton.

"Damn it!"

Archer understood that Dylan intended for Salazzle to attack them, rendering them unable to resist.

But how could he let that happen?!

Buzz, buzz, buzz~!

At that moment, the faint sound of wings flapping reached Dylan's ears. Nidoking and Salazzle noticed it immediately as well.

Dylan looked towards the left corridor, his expression tense. A yellow figure, as fast as lightning, was swiftly approaching.

"Salazzle!"

Despite Dylan's warning and Salazzle quickly gathering flames in its mouth, it was still too late.

Bang!

The yellow Pokémon, like a knight's lance, thrust its long needle fiercely into Salazzle.

Salazzle felt a sharp pain and immense force hit her, sending her flying backward like a cannonball, smashing heavily into the wall behind her, shattering it.

Everything happened too fast! So fast that the three present hadn't even reacted yet.

This...

Dylan's brow furrowed tightly as he looked at the Pokémon that had stopped in front of Archer and Proton.

It resembled a giant yellow wasp, with two pairs of wings on its back and slender legs, with large, long white needles at the front of its arms, resembling the head of a knight's lance.

It was a Beedrill, a Bug/Poison-type Pokémon!

Beedrill was one of the most common Bug-type Pokémon in the Kanto region. You could find swarms of them in almost any large forest, though they were usually not very strong.

However, this Beedrill had just swiftly attacked and sent Dylan's Salazzle flying!

Even though Salazzle wasn't Dylan's main Pokémon, it was already at level 53, making it quite powerful among the Elite Four-level Pokémon.

But it was so easily sent flying by this Beedrill!

"Boss is here!" As high-ranking Team Rocket executives, Archer and Proton were delighted to see the Beedrill.

The debris was pushed aside, and Salazzle slowly emerged, breathing heavily, her narrow eyes glaring angrily at Beedrill.

It was clear that Salazzle wasn't in good shape.

"Tsk~ What a strong Beedrill. Are you not going to show yourself?" Dylan licked his dry lips as he spoke.

Dylan glanced at Salazzle. It seemed that Beedrill hadn't even used any special move, meaning it was just a regular attack that had done this much damage to Salazzle.

Such a terrifying Beedrill could only belong to one person—Giovanni, the boss of Team Rocket!

Click, click, click~

The sound of polished shoes echoed, and from around the corner, a middle-aged man in a black suit and fedora hat slowly walked out.

It was Giovanni!

"Heh~ Young people these days are truly amazing."

A deep, hoarse male voice came from him, clearly disguised, as were his appearance and build.

Dylan looked Giovanni up and down. He was very different from how he remembered him, but that made sense.

Giovanni appearing here in person was already incredibly risky. If he hadn't disguised himself, his Viridian Gym would've been raided overnight.

For a base in Celadon City that was bound to be destroyed, Giovanni wouldn't have acted so recklessly.

"And you are?" Dylan placed his left hand in his pocket, pressing a button on his communicator. He was signaling Lance, something they had agreed upon before the operation.

As fast as he was growing, Dylan knew his limits. He was still far from the level of top Champions like Giovanni or Lance.

This Beedrill in front of him was at least level 80! And that was a conservative estimate.

"Boss!" Archer and Proton immediately ran to Giovanni's side, both respectful and ashamed.

They never imagined that one day, they would need their boss to personally come to their rescue. As subordinates, they felt like total failures.

"You should understand now," Giovanni said, taking the USB drive from Archer and slipping it into his pocket, his mature and calm smile directed at Dylan.

In fact, Giovanni had been here for a while. If it weren't for the fact that Archer and Proton had no way to escape, he wouldn't have risked stepping in.

"The leader of Team Rocket," Dylan squinted slightly.

"Exactly, but today really isn't a good day. Kid, if we get the chance, we'll meet again," Giovanni said calmly, preparing to leave with Archer and Proton.

Although he found Dylan intriguing, the Kanto Champion, Lance, was just outside, and Giovanni didn't want to deal with him right now.

Beedrill floated behind the three, its red insect eyes fixed on Dylan.

"I can't let you leave so easily," Dylan decisively threw out a Pokéball.

"Articuno, Blizzard!"

"Yiao~!"

Beautiful ice-blue light gleamed on Articuno's body as it unleashed a powerful snowstorm.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

The air in the base plummeted in temperature, white snowflakes fell, and the walls on both sides of the corridor frosted over.

A very powerful Blizzard!

"Oh?" Giovanni paused, surprised as he turned around. Articuno?

But as the raging snowstorm approached, Beedrill simply raised its right limb, and a white light flashed.

A Fighting-type Brick Break!

With an impressive strike from its long, white needle, the Blizzard was effortlessly split in two.

Watching Beedrill handle the attack as if it were slicing through vegetables, Dylan couldn't help but be amazed. This Beedrill was ridiculously strong!

There was no way he could beat this absurdly powerful Beedrill, but it didn't matter. He had bought enough time.

Boom!

A loud explosion erupted from nearby, flames bursting out as a thick wall was blasted open.

A black cloak billowed as a stern-faced red-haired young man walked in, a powerful Dragonite following closely behind.

Lance!

Giovanni narrowed his eyes as he looked at the arrogant, cold-blooded redhead.

This was going to be interesting!

Dylan took a few steps back, leaning against Articuno as he watched Giovanni and Lance get closer. He was just about ready to grab some popcorn.

The clash between Kanto's Champion, Lance, and the dark mastermind of Team Rocket, Giovanni—this was going to be one hell of a show!

(End of Chapter)

# 81: Chapter 81: Is This the Best Your Dragon Can Do?

"Boss?"

Both Archer and Proton's expressions changed slightly when they saw Lance arrive.

Team Rocket dominates the underworld of the entire Viridian City, essentially making them the kings of Kanto's dark world. However, the Kanto League looms over them like a mountain, forcing everyone in Team Rocket to live underground.

Within the Kanto League, two people are most feared by Team Rocket: Lorelei and Lance!

"Don't panic, you two retreat first and look for a chance to escape," Giovanni said calmly.

"But boss..."

Archer and Proton were a bit reluctant. Did their boss really have to cover their retreat?

"Lance is a madman. Whether he will attack you or not, I can't guarantee. Just fall back."

Giovanni watched as Lance approached—a man slowly descending into madness. Lance, the Dragon Master, had been driven to the edge by the sight of Team Rocket's genetic experiments over the years and by the corruption within the League itself. In Giovanni's eyes, Lance was like a mad dog growing more unhinged, but the most terrifying thing was that this mad dog was incredibly powerful.

As he thought of the possible scenarios, Giovanni's lips curled up slightly.

"Yes, sir."

Archer and Proton responded solemnly and began to slowly retreat.

Meanwhile...

"Lance, sir."

Dylan approached Lance.

"You captured Articuno?"

Lance nodded at Dylan, glancing at the Articuno behind him with some surprise.

"Yes, just a couple of days ago, by sheer luck," Dylan replied with a smile.

"Not bad," Lance said, a faint smile of approval on his lips. Dylan was a junior he acknowledged, and seeing him grow stronger gave Lance comfort. He was confident that Dylan would one day challenge him!

"Archer, Proton... and this guy?"

Lance crossed his arms, his expression turning cold as his indifferent gaze swept over Archer and Proton, finally settling on Giovanni.

Lance's sharp eyes narrowed slightly. His instincts told him this man was not simple.

"Lance, sir, this is the leader of Team Rocket," Dylan whispered.

Team Rocket's leader?!!

Lance's pupils suddenly dilated, and the muscles on his face twitched strangely. It was as if he wanted to smile but was holding back, creating an eerie and terrifying expression.

Staring intently at the middle-aged man in a black suit, his blood-red hair partially covering his murderous eyes, Lance finally spoke.

"Finally showing your face, you filthy rat!"

After a long silence, Lance's lips curled into a wide, menacing grin, revealing his teeth in a way that would frighten even children.

At this moment, he looked less like the Champion of the Kanto Elite Four and more like a sinister villain.

"Lance really has some psychological issues," Dylan thought as he stepped back, his gaze flickering as he watched the now terrifying Lance. In the original story, Lance was a twisted and fanatical villain, but in this merged world, Lance had become the Champion of Kanto.

Though he usually seemed merely aloof and cold, his extreme emotions were now becoming evident.

Lance had been born in Viridian Forest and had received its blessings. He loved nature and Pokémon deeply. However, sometimes, excessive love can lead to twisted, unhealthy behavior.

Lance's intolerance of corruption made him despise Team Rocket, especially for their cruel genetic experiments on innocent Pokémon.

To ordinary people in this world, Team Rocket's ruthless actions warranted life sentences in prison for their leaders. But to Lance, prison was too lenient. Anyone associated with Team Rocket, anyone wearing their uniform, deserved nothing less than death!

And now, the leader of Team Rocket, the source of Kanto's darkness, was standing right in front of him.

Lance felt a surge of excitement!

"Dragonite! Tear him apart!"

With a crazed smile, Lance's eyes, partly obscured by his blood-red hair, seemed to be shrouded in darkness. At his command, the unusually grim Dragonite behind him shot towards Giovanni like a yellow blur.

Dragon Claw!

Dragonite's razor-sharp claws sliced through the air, aiming straight for Giovanni.

Bang!!

But in the next moment, a sharp needle intercepted Dragonite's Dragon Claw, stopping it in its tracks. It was Beedrill.

"Hmm?" Lance tilted his head slightly.

He hadn't paid much attention to this Beedrill at first. After all, how could such a weak Pokémon stand against a dragon? But for it to block Dragonite's Dragon Claw so easily was no ordinary feat.

"Seems like you've got some skill after all, you filthy rat," Lance sneered, his grin widening as he looked at Giovanni, who was shielded by Beedrill.

"I'll accept your praise, Lance," Giovanni said, tipping his black hat slightly, his low, raspy voice carrying an odd elegance.

"But I'm surprised. Is this really the strength of the 'invincible' Dragon Master, Lance?"

Giovanni's lips curled into a faint, mocking smile as he spoke slowly.

"Is this the best your dragon can do?"

Is this the best your dragon can do?

Lance's facial muscles twitched again, as if torn between laughter and rage.

Tsk~ Giovanni's words were sharp, indeed.

Dylan couldn't help but click his tongue. Everyone knew that Lance took great pride in his dragon army.

All of Lance's pride stemmed from his dragons! His unbeatable dragons in Kanto!

"Haha~ hahahaha~!!"

Suddenly, Lance's maniacal laughter echoed through the hallway.

But in the next instant, the laughter abruptly stopped, and Lance's expression turned ice-cold.

"Then let's see the power of this dragon you call 'no big deal.'"

"Just don't run away with your tail between your legs, you rat hiding in the sewers!"

"Heh~"

Giovanni chuckled softly, unfazed by Lance's taunt.

He subtly signaled to Archer and Proton, indicating that they should leave while they had the chance.

Archer and Proton exchanged glances before nodding to Giovanni and preparing to retreat.

"Hmph~ Do you think you can escape?"

Of course, Lance noticed Archer and Proton's movements, but he wasn't too concerned about those two small fry.

"Dylan, go after them. It'll be good for you."

Lance's eyes remained fixed on Giovanni and Beedrill as he spoke coldly.

He didn't care much for Archer and Proton, but Dylan was different. If Dylan could capture Archer and Proton, along with Ariana from before, the benefits he'd gain would be immense.

Seriously, even in front of Team Rocket's leader, Lance was still thinking about Dylan's future.

But...

Dylan was torn. He really wanted to stay and watch Lance and Giovanni's showdown! This was a rare, epic battle.

"Alright, leave it to me, Lance," Dylan reluctantly agreed. He couldn't find a good excuse to stay.

Before leaving, however, Dylan set up a camera drone, hoping it would survive to capture the fight.

He hadn't gone far when he heard the sounds of a fierce battle. Clearly, Lance and Giovanni had started fighting.

Meanwhile...

Using a hidden passage, Archer and Proton emerged onto a small street.

"I hope the boss is okay," Proton said, worried.

"Don't worry, let's just leave Celadon City for now. Move," Archer replied with a grim expression. This mission had been a complete failure.

But he couldn't understand how Dylan had become so strong!

As they stepped out of the building and rounded a corner, Archer froze.

Wearing a hat with clear, red eyes visible under the brim, dressed in a red jacket and blue jeans, stood Red!

"..."

Red was also momentarily surprised to see Archer and Proton, but he quickly understood the situation.

Their disheveled appearance indicated they had escaped from Team Rocket's base.

Two flashes of light appeared as Charizard and Pikachu took their places in front of Red.

Red adjusted his hat, his expression unreadable. He had received a distress call from Leah earlier, but he had been too far from Celadon City at the time.

By the time he arrived, the League had already launched its attack, and Officer Jenny wouldn't allow him to assist. So, he had been patrolling the area when he stumbled upon the fleeing Archer and Proton.

Well, thank you, Mother Nature.

"Isn't that the kid from the ship last time?" Proton whispered to Archer.

Archer's eyelid twitched. Of course, he knew.

"Relax! Archer, you're the top Executive of Team Rocket! This kid can't be as tough as Dylan. If we defeat him, we can escape. You can do it!"

Proton encouraged Archer, though he had no choice—his main Pokémon had all been defeated by Dylan. He couldn't exactly fistfight Charizard.

Stop jinxing me, you idiot!

Archer suppressed the urge to smack Proton as he took out two Poké Balls. He had to focus on defeating this obstacle first.

"Red! Let's settle this!"

That's right, Red wasn't Dylan. There was no way he had gone easy on them back on the ship, right?

They had to win!

(End of Chapter)

---

---

---

# 82: Chapter 82: I Lost Again

When Dylan arrived at the alley, the battle had just ended.

Proton looked helpless, while Archer was in shock, standing there dumbfounded.

Yes, Archer had lost again.

Because his core team of Houndoom and Weezing had already been defeated by Dylan, his combat power was incomplete.

Moreover, compared to the last time on the Battle, Red's combat experience and strength had increased significantly.

The battle between the two was intense, but undoubtedly, Archer had been defeated once more.

"I... I lost again..."

Looking at his Crobat and Victreebel, both incapacitated, Archer fell into deep self-doubt.

This doesn't make sense!

During the last encounter on the Battle, hadn't he fought both Dylan and Red to a standstill? How did things turn out like this now?

"Brother, in the future, don't try to act tough in front of us," Proton said, patting Archer on the shoulder with a heavy heart.

It wasn't even about Dylan, but this Red—Archer couldn't even solo him!

How could he have the nerve to say he almost won that two-on-one?

"???"

Archer felt his face heat up rapidly, and he didn't know if he should cough up the blood in his throat or not.

Archer's face turned an ugly shade, but there was nothing he could do. He noticed Dylan walking towards them from the other side.

"Yo~ Red, so you're here," Dylan greeted Red.

Red: (=v=)

Red nodded slightly, but it was clear he was in a good mood, happy to reunite with Dylan.

"Mr. Archer, Mr. Proton, I didn't expect that even with your boss giving you a chance to retreat, you still failed," Dylan teased as he looked at Archer and Proton.

Neither Archer nor Proton responded. The only consolation for Archer was that he had managed to pass on the gene fusion research data to Giovanni.

Rumble!!

At that moment, the ground suddenly shook violently, the tremors even reaching Dylan and Red.

A yellow figure suddenly burst out from the underground in the distance, then quickly sped off into the distance.

The next moment, Lance's Dragonite soared into the sky, scanning the area before locking onto a direction and giving chase.

"Is that?" Red's red eyes flickered as he looked at Dylan.

"That's the leader of Team Rocket, a very strong trainer. Lance is currently battling him," Dylan replied after a moment of thought.

"But it seems that the Team Rocket leader is about to flee."

There was no doubt about it; Giovanni wouldn't engage Lance in a prolonged battle under these circumstances. After ensuring that Archer and Proton were safe enough, Giovanni would undoubtedly retreat as well.

The leader of Team Rocket, huh? Red remained silent. He had no fondness for the evil Team Rocket, and naturally, their leader was the source of all evil.

If they ever met, he would do everything in his power to capture him!

With Archer and Proton captured, the operation to clear out the Team Rocket base in Celadon City was a huge success.

Including Ariana, who was captured before the operation, they had effectively captured three of Team Rocket's high-ranking officers in one go!

"Thank you for your hard work, Gym Leader Dylan, and to both of you as well. We greatly appreciate your assistance."

Outside the Game Corner, Archer and Proton had their Poké Balls confiscated and were handcuffed. Officer Jenny, in charge of the operation, rarely smiled as she expressed her gratitude to Dylan and the others.

Leah and Red both shook their heads, as usual, trying to keep a low profile. Leah didn't want to stand out, and Red simply wasn't interested.

"Officer Jenny, you're too kind. As a member of the League, it's everyone's duty to eliminate the evil Team Rocket," Dylan responded politely. He wasn't after verbal thanks; he wanted something more tangible.

"You truly deserve the praise, Gym Leader Dylan. As the key contributor to this operation, I'm sure the League will reward you handsomely," Officer Jenny said with a smile.

Indeed, although Red had intercepted the two and defeated Archer, securing them, the credit was largely given to Dylan. Red, being modest and shy, didn't like being in the spotlight and once again pushed his achievements to Dylan, just like on the S.S. Anne.

"It was really a group effort…" Dylan was about to say more when his phone rang. Seeing that it was Lance calling, he answered.

"Lance," Dylan answered.

"Dylan, I've already heard the report from Officer Jenny. You did a great job. I've informed the President, and he'll likely contact you personally."

Lance's calm voice came through the phone.

"Thank you, Lance. What about the Team Rocket leader?" Dylan was still curious about the outcome of the battle between Lance and Giovanni.

"That rat managed to escape," Lance replied coldly.

Indeed, he hadn't caught up with Giovanni. The speed of Giovanni's Beedrill was astonishing, and with it being nighttime, it was even harder for Lance to track him down. In the end, he lost him, which infuriated Lance. It took him a while to calm down before contacting Dylan.

"After all, he's the leader of Team Rocket, incredibly cunning," Dylan offered a gentle consolation.

"Hmph! I won't let him escape next time," Lance huffed.

After ending the call with Lance, Dylan couldn't help but feel a bit disappointed. The camera he had left in the Team Rocket base was now in pieces, evidently destroyed during the battle between Lance and Giovanni.

Leaving the post-battle cleanup to Officer Jenny and her team, Dylan and the others left early.

"You guys encountered the leader of Team Rocket?" Leah asked lightly, clearly in high spirits after successfully dismantling a major Team Rocket base.

"Yes, he's incredibly strong. We must be very cautious if we encounter him again," Dylan nodded. Giovanni was one of the strongest in every version, second only to Rainbow Giovanni in "Ultra Sun and Moon" and the Giovanni in the Special series.

Today, Dylan had witnessed firsthand the terrifying power of Giovanni's ace, Beedrill.

Among his current team, the strongest was Articuno, yet even Articuno's full-force Blizzard had been effortlessly cleaved in two by Beedrill's simple Brick Break…

It was truly ridiculous.

"Haha~ Don't worry! I believe in you and Red. You two will definitely be able to defeat that big boss in the future!"

Leah quickly moved ahead of the two, smiling brightly as she looked up at them with her large eyes sparkling with unknown intent.

Can we do it?

Red tilted his head, a bit confused. He felt like Leah's words had a deeper meaning.

Dylan, however, understood who Leah's 'big boss' was referring to, and after a moment of silence, he chuckled.

"We can."

Yes, given enough time, of course they could!

"Hehe~"

Though Leah believed that neither Dylan nor Red understood her true meaning, she was still delighted by Dylan's affirmation.

"In that case, as a reward, I'll give you guys this spoils of war~"

Leah extended her fair hand to them, revealing a shrunken Poké Ball in her palm.

"What is this?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"I found it in the base's lab. Team Rocket truly committed unforgivable sins." Leah's expression revealed a hint of disgust and sadness. Even with her strong mind, the sights in that lab had made her nauseous and horrified.

"This poor creature deserves better. I believe that either you or Red will be able to care for it properly and end its suffering."

Leah pursed her lips, speaking earnestly to the two.

"I've already had other gains, and you both gave up the League rewards for me. Red?"

Dylan pondered for a moment and then looked at Red. He had already encountered an unclaimed Darkrai and would try to catch it. Moreover, Leah and Red had pushed all the credit for this mission onto him, so taking another Pokémon didn't feel right.

They were friends, and Dylan wasn't a greedy person.

"I didn't do much," Red shook his head, refusing. After some back-and-forth, Red still stubbornly declined the Pokémon, leaving Dylan to look at Leah again.

Leah raised an eyebrow and firmly placed the Poké Ball in Dylan's hand.

"Alright, alright. I'll prepare other gifts for you two later," Dylan relented.

"Then we'll look forward to it, Mr. Big Shot of Silph Co.," Leah said in a playful, mockingly sweet voice, making Red shiver with goosebumps.

"Leah? What Pokémon is this?" Dylan asked, amused and curious as he looked at the Poké Ball.

"You'll see when you get home. This one's special."

(End of Chapter)

---

---

---

# 83: Chapter 83: Is Our Larvitar Inferior to Them?

The Kanto League launched an operation to take down a large Team Rocket base in Celadon City, personally led by the Champion of the Elite Four, Lance.

This news quickly spread throughout the Kanto region, and there was no way to keep it under wraps. After all, the evacuation of civilians around the Game Corner caused quite a commotion.

Not to mention the noise from the battle that followed, but in the end, it was a great victory. Naturally, the Pokémon League had no intention of suppressing the spread of this event.

Especially since the last time the S.S. Anne was hijacked by Team Rocket, it had a significantly negative impact. Team Rocket's audacity was seen as a blatant disregard for the Kanto League, causing concern among the people.

But now, not long after, the Kanto League had wiped out a large Team Rocket base. This was undoubtedly the Kanto League's counterattack!

The forums under the Kanto League were buzzing with posts discussing this event, generating a lot of heat.

Back at the hotel, Dylan first took a shower, and when he came out, he noticed an unfamiliar call.

"Hello, who is this?"

Dylan sat on a chair, playing with the Poké Ball that Leah had given him, and spoke politely.

"Hehe~ Gym Leader Dylan, Lance should have mentioned me to you," a kind and elderly voice came through.

Dylan immediately recognized it.

"President Charles Goodshow?"

Charles Goodshow, the current President of the Kanto League and also the Chairman of the Pokémon League, was considered one of the greatest figures in the Pokémon League, reflecting the respect people had for him.

"Yes, it's this old man. Dylan, Lance thinks very highly of you."

Charles Goodshow teased with a smile. Lance was known for being extremely aloof, rarely giving anyone the time of day.

"I'm also very grateful for Lance's guidance," Dylan nodded. It was evident that Lance had indeed looked out for him.

However, Dylan seriously suspected that Lance was just waiting for someone like himself, who had also been recognized by the Viridian Forest, to challenge him.

And then what? He would undoubtedly lose to Lance's dragon army, allowing Lance to use his seniority to educate him that "the power of dragons is invincible!"

Well, in Lance's eyes, Dylan was just someone who used Poison-type Pokémon.

"Lance was personally brought into the Kanto League by me, and in all these years, no one has ever been treated so specially by him. He even made a request to me on your behalf."

Charles was a bit amused and a bit gratified. Lance had become increasingly cold and extreme, gradually immersing himself in his own world.

Charles was actually very worried. He even had a bad premonition that if this continued, Lance might step into an abyss from which he could not return.

But now it seemed that Lance still had some "humanity" left in him; it was just that ordinary people couldn't gain his approval.

"..." Dylan awkwardly chuckled.

Lance was a bit too straightforward; he actually made the request for him?

"Alright, Dylan, let's talk about your rewards for capturing the three high-ranking Team Rocket members and taking down their base."

Sensing Dylan's embarrassment, Charles kindly changed the subject.

"Considering your achievements, a single pseudo-legendary Pokémon might not be enough as a reward. According to Lance, you wanted a Larvitar, right?"

Charles asked with a smile.

"Yes, President. If a Bagon or Gible is available, that would be fine too. A Shiny Beldum would be even better."

"Hehe~ Dylan, you sure know how to challenge an old man like me."

Charles chuckled. Ordinary pseudo-legendary Pokémon were one thing, but Dylan was asking for one with exceptional potential, which was already hard to come by.

A Bagon or Gible with excellent potential was highly prized, even by the Hoenn and Sinnoh Leagues.

"As for a Shiny Beldum... Dylan~ even if you sold me, I wouldn't be able to get one for you, so don't even think about it."

Charles was quite humorous. It wasn't that he lacked the resources to obtain a Shiny Beldum, but it was just something that depended on luck.

Shiny Pokémon were already extremely rare, and Charles hadn't heard of any Shiny pseudo-legendaries appearing recently.

"As for Bagon or Gible, there's no problem, but do you think our Larvitar is inferior to them?"

Although he was the highly respected President of the Pokémon League, at the end of the day, he was still the President of the Kanto League. Was his region's pseudo-legendary inferior to others?

Was Tyranitar any weaker than Salamence or Garchomp?

"Not at all, President. I'm very fond of Tyranitar," Dylan quickly understood what Charles meant and responded with a smile.

"I can give you a Larvitar, and as an additional reward for your significant achievements, I can also give you a Shiny Pokémon."

"I got that little one from an old friend, and it took quite a bit of effort, so don't let it down."

Charles joked kindly. He hadn't had a good night's sleep in days trying to secure this Shiny Pokémon.

"Thank you, President!" Dylan's eyes lit up.

A Shiny Pokémon, especially one personally obtained by Charles, had to be quite rare.

And with an exceptionally talented Larvitar, this was a huge win! No wonder the Pokémon League was so wealthy!

"Hehe~ If you want to thank me, then keep working hard. The future of the League depends on young people like you."

Charless old face lit up with a bright smile. He loved seeing vibrant young people; they represented the League's bright future.

"Come visit the Indigo Plateau sometime, and give my regards to Itto. That guy really left you with a mess to deal with."

After some more small talk, Charles complained about Dylan's father, Itto.

As the largest shareholder of Silph Co., Itto had crossed paths with Charles several times.

"Haha~ But I have to thank my dad too. If he hadn't pushed me, I wouldn't have gotten stronger," Dylan laughed heartily.

"That's true~ Young people need pressure to grow. Alright then, Dylan, I'll send those two Pokémon to you tomorrow."

"Got it, President. Goodbye."

After hanging up with Charles Goodshow, Dylan was in a great mood.

He dried his hair, changed into a clean set of clothes, and headed to a nearby training ground.

"Come on out."

Looking at the two Poké Balls in his hand, Dylan decided to release the one Leah had given him first.

With a flash of white light, a small, fox-like Pokémon appeared before him.

Most of its fur was brown, with cream-colored fur around its neck and tail, and it had long, rabbit-like ears.

"Eevee?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow at the little creature.

Yes, it was indeed an Eevee.

Suddenly, Dylan remembered that in the Special manga, there was an Eevee that Team Rocket had experimented on, allowing it to evolve into Electric, Fire, and Water types at will and revert back to Eevee.

"Eevee..."

The Eevee cautiously observed its surroundings, keeping a distance from Dylan.

"Don't be afraid; we're no longer in that base. No one will ever experiment on you again."

Dylan squatted down slightly, speaking gently to Eevee, and let his Viridian power seep out, calming Eevee's anxious emotions.

"Eevee..."

The soothing effect of the Viridian power was significant, conveying Dylan's goodwill to Eevee. Its emotions gradually settled, and it didn't resist Dylan as much.

"Good girl, are you hungry?" Dylan carefully approached Eevee, offering it some Pokéblocks.

Eevee was still a bit wary, its cute nose twitching as it sniffed, its little feet fidgeting as it wanted to approach but seemed hesitant.

"Don't be afraid, this is for you. Go ahead and eat," Dylan's voice was extremely gentle, and his kind eyes gave Eevee the courage it needed.

"Eevee~"

Eevee gently licked the Pokéblock and seemed to enjoy the taste, happily nibbling on two blocks.

"Don't worry, I won't let anyone hurt you again."

Dylan gently patted Eevee's small head; this little one was truly adorable.

"Little one, how about wearing this? It will stop the pain."

After some thought, Dylan took out a necklace with an Everstone and gently suggested it to Eevee.

Yes, although this Eevee had the power to evolve and revert at will, the process wasn't perfect. Each evolution caused it pain.

So, in the Special manga, Red eventually evolved Eevee into an Espeon, freeing it from that pain forever.

Eevee had finished the Pokéblocks and licked its lips, its gaze curious as it tilted its head at the Everstone necklace.

"Wear this, and it won't hurt anymore." Dylan placed the necklace around Eevee's neck.

What form should it evolve into?

Watching Eevee curiously play with the Everstone, Dylan pondered.

Although Eevee could evolve into three different forms, each with its own combat style, this ability caused it too much pain.

Dylan couldn't bear to see his Pokémon suffer like that.

Aside from Water, Fire, and Electric forms, the other five types—Psychic, Dark, Grass, Ice, and Fairy—each had their merits.

---

---

# 84: Chapter 84: You're Not Weak!

'Beep~ Eevee, Normal Type, Evolution Pokémon

Ability: Adaptability

Moves: Tackle, Tail Whip, Quick Attack, Swift, Bite, Detect (inherited)

Description: To quickly adapt to changes in its environment, this Pokémon possesses the potential for multiple evolutions.'

"Little one, are you full?" Dylan picked up the now much more familiar Eevee, placing it on his shoulder, and gently rubbed its fluffy chin.

"Vee~!" Eevee's adorable little face showed a human-like happy smile, and it nuzzled against Dylan's cheek with a crisp cry.

For some reason, it couldn't help but feel close to this human before it, even though it had sworn it would never easily forgive humans.

"Treats are fine, but you need to control the amount every day," Dylan said, taking out a special meat treat and offering it to Eevee.

"Vee~ Vee~" Eevee bit into it, chewing contentedly with its eyes adorably squinted into crescent shapes.

Dylan smiled as he watched the joyful little Eevee. He felt a tinge of regret; this Eevee did not have its hidden ability, otherwise, he would have loved to evolve it into a Sylveon. Sylveon with Pixilate is very powerful; without the hidden ability, it's still usable but lacks a certain flair. Umbreon is also a good option since it doesn't rely heavily on its ability, and Synchronize Umbreon is quite effective. Plus, Dylan was currently building a Dark-type team.

However, he decided to let this little one choose for itself in the future. Dylan thought for a moment and decided to respect Eevee's own wishes. This Pokémon had already been through enough due to Team Rocket's experiments, which had caused it to be capable of evolving into three forms. Its evolution should be its own choice.

After bonding with Eevee for a while, Dylan took out another Poké Ball; this one was the heavyweight.

"Come out." Dylan opened the Poké Ball.

With a flash of white light, a pitch-black shadow rose slowly in front of Dylan. It was Darkrai.

Compared to its weakened state at the Team Rocket base, Darkrai had recovered significantly, but it still seemed quite weary. Who knew what it had been through?

"Darkrai, hello, I'm Dylan. Do you remember me?" Dylan smiled at Darkrai, the Eevee on his shoulder chewing its treat more quietly, curiously tilting its head to look at the nightmare Pokémon.

Darkrai remained silent. Of course, it remembered; it had seen Dylan crush that annoying green-haired human. And it was Dylan who had saved it from that vault.

"Although I don't know how Team Rocket caught you, from now on, Darkrai, you are free. You have escaped from Team Rocket," Dylan continued. Darkrai, as a mythical Pokémon from Sinnoh, appearing in a Team Rocket base was too outrageous. Although Dylan had his suspicions, they were just guesses. It was clear Darkrai had suffered a lot at Team Rocket's hands.

Darkrai remained silent, Dylan's words stirring unpleasant memories.

"The past is the past, Darkrai," Dylan noticed Darkrai's gloomy mood and quickly changed the topic. "What do you plan to do next?"

Darkrai's blue eyes showed a hint of confusion. Plan? What plan could it have? Its trainer had abandoned it; it didn't even know where to go.

"This is probably a burden for you too." Dylan pressed the release button and let go of the red and white Poké Ball. The ball rolled to Darkrai's feet, its blue eyes flickering.

"I don't know what you've been through, Darkrai. Maybe now isn't the best time," Dylan said, taking out a shrunken dark red Poké Ball. "But I'm the kind of person who likes to try things, Darkrai. I want to capture you."

Dylan smiled warmly at Darkrai. Darkrai looked complexly at the human who had saved it; it had witnessed this human's strength firsthand.

'I... I'm very weak...' a deep, raspy voice suddenly sounded in Dylan's ear.

Dylan's eyes brightened. This was Darkrai's telepathy! Most mythical and legendary Pokémon could use this special ability, and some particularly special Pokémon could learn it too. It allowed them to communicate directly through the mind, bypassing language barriers.

Darkrai's species indeed used telepathy. Thinking of a certain movie featuring Darkrai, Dylan felt at ease.

"Weak? How can you be weak, Darkrai?" Dylan chuckled. He liked this telepathic Darkrai even more. "You are a mythical Pokémon. How many people in the world seek your power? Including me."

"..." Darkrai's blue eyes showed a trace of sadness. Yes, it knew many humans sought its power. But so what? It had still been abandoned because it wasn't strong enough.

"Even if something special happened, Darkrai, you are not weak. Come with me." Dylan extended his hand to Darkrai, his tone sincere. "No matter what happened in the past, become my partner. Starting tomorrow, you will be reborn! Everyone will see, Darkrai, you are not weak. I will help you become strong. I will stand with you against those who hurt you and make them regret it!"

Darkrai's blue eyes flickered with emotion. Unknowingly, the pitch-black nightmare gradually approached the young man before it.

"I want... to become strong..."

"Yes, I will help you become strong. We will be great partners, Darkrai~" Dylan smiled as he looked at the dark red Premier Ball in his hand. What a wonderful day it was. Darkrai, captured!

(End of Chapter)

# 85: Chapter 85: So Disappointing, Lance

'Ding~ Darkrai, Dark Type, Dark Pokémon

Ability: Nightmare

Moves: Dark Void, Night Shade, Feint Attack, Hypnosis, Ice Beam, Psychic, Dark Pulse…

Description: It is said that Darkrai brings terrifying nightmares on moonless nights.'

Looking at Darkrai's information in the Pokédex, Dylan fell into thought.

According to the game, Dark Void is a move Darkrai can only learn at level 66.

However, based on the moves listed in the Pokédex, this Darkrai clearly hasn't reached level 66 yet.

Since Darkrai hasn't even learned Double Team, does this mean that in reality, legendary Pokémon can learn their exclusive moves earlier?

Or perhaps another Darkrai taught this one, allowing it to learn Dark Void ahead of time?

Dylan couldn't be sure, but it wasn't a major issue. He hugged his cute Eevee and returned to the hotel.

Gym Leader Group Chat.

Celadon Gym—Erika: Phew~ What a great victory! Everyone, today's operation was a huge success! (Happy.jpg)

Celadon Gym—Erika: Executive Archer and Proton were both captured! Hehe~ Dylan, you're amazing!

Pewter Gym—Flint: Impressive! (ThumbsUp.jpg)

Cerulean Gym—Lily: We saw the announcement too, wow~! (Cheering.jpg)

Vermilion Gym—Lt. Surge: Hahaha~ You guys are awesome, Archer and Proton are such losers! (Laughing.jpg)

Fuchsia Gym—Koga: My respect.

Cinnabar Gym—Blaine: Haha~ Young people these days really make us old folks look bad~ (Cool.jpg)

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: Heh~ It's a pity I couldn't leave my place; otherwise, I would have loved to witness Dylan and Champion Lance in action firsthand. (Regret.jpg)

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (HeadTilt.jpg)

Rota Gym—Dylan: With everyone's efforts, the evil Team Rocket couldn't resist. It's a pity we didn't capture their leader. If only Giovanni and the other veterans were there, the leader wouldn't have escaped. (Sigh.jpg)

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: It's indeed a pity. Next time we take action against Team Rocket, I'll definitely join. Everyone should learn from Erika and investigate if there are any Team Rocket bases in their cities.

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (Surprised.jpg)

Pewter Gym—Flint: As expected of Giovanni! I'll contact Officer Jenny tomorrow to start a thorough investigation.

Cerulean Gym—Daisy: Oh no~ But we three sisters aren't strong enough. If we find anything, can we ask for everyone's help? (Tearful.jpg)

On a small path in Celadon City, Giovanni smiled as he read Daisy's message and replied affirmatively.

Beside Giovanni stood a girl as delicate as a doll.

She had long, straight black hair reaching down to her legs, an expressionless face that was extremely fair and exquisite, a form-fitting red dress with a black belt cinching her slender waist, and black tights covering her long legs.

She was a stunning girl.

"Truly disappointing."

Giovanni looked at the police station in the distance, his calm, deep voice betraying no emotion.

Although the data from the gene fusion experiment was successfully retrieved, three of his four Executives were captured!

How could he accept this?

The Beasts Trio couldn't be easily mobilized from Johto, and the three Admins were too unique to be readily deployed. Giovanni usually only had the four Executives at his disposal.

And now, with three Executives gone, he hardly had anyone left in Kanto!

"Prepare yourself, Sabrina."

"Yes, boss."

The girl named Sabrina put her phone away and released an Alakazam.

"Next, the Kanto Alliance will undoubtedly increase their efforts against Team Rocket."

Giovanni said calmly. No matter what, after tonight's actions, the Kanto Alliance would be furious.

After all, Team Rocket had attacked the Celadon City police station and rescued their captured Executives.

But it couldn't be helped. By tomorrow, Archer and Proton would be secretly transported to the Indigo Plateau for detention, a place even Giovanni dared not easily infiltrate.

Moreover, this was part of Giovanni's plan.

He wanted the Alliance to be furious, especially one particular person.

"Alakazam, Hypnosis." Sabrina commanded coldly.

Her Alakazam's Hypnosis was extremely powerful. Without any preparation, most of the police station's personnel would be put to sleep.

Giovanni, in disguise, could easily enter and rescue Archer and Proton, though Ariana had already been sent to the Indigo Plateau in the afternoon.

...

Early the next morning, Dylan was woken up by a phone call.

"Dylan! Bad news, I just got word that the Celadon police station was attacked last night!"

Erika said hurriedly.

"And the Team Rocket leader left a message too."

"?" Dylan was confused.

After a while, Dylan finally understood the situation.

At ten o'clock last night, an Alakazam appeared and used Hypnosis to put most of the police station to sleep. Then, the Team Rocket leader, along with a Beedrill, easily entered the station and rescued the specially detained Archer and Proton.

Giovanni even left a message on the police station wall!

"Tsk~ The Alliance must be furious."

Ending the call with Erika, Dylan clicked his tongue.

Good grief, Giovanni's direct assault on the Celadon police station to rescue his people was a blatant slap in the face for the Kanto Alliance, especially with the taunting message he left behind.

The Kanto Alliance couldn't not be angry, especially Lance.

Dylan had asked Erika what Giovanni had written, and upon learning the answer, he was certain Giovanni did it on purpose.

But… this was getting interesting.

Dylan got up and dressed. The conflict between Team Rocket and the Kanto Alliance was intensifying.

Indigo Plateau Conference Room.

The Kanto Elite Four and Alliance senior council members were gathered here.

"Outrageous!! How dare Team Rocket act so brazenly!"

"Too arrogant! They're blatantly challenging the authority of the Kanto Alliance!"

"First they hijacked the S.S. Anne, and now they've attacked a police station. Is there nothing they won't do?"

"Lawless! Simply lawless!"

The conference room was in an uproar, usually composed council members now red-faced with anger.

Charles Goodshow sat at the head of the table, his gray eyebrows furrowed.

Lance stared intently at the large screen in front of him, which displayed the message in the Celadon police station lobby.

'Your dragon is nothing special.'

Lance's handsome face twisted and trembled, his mouth twitching as if he wanted to laugh but also seemed angry, his expression extremely strange.

Lorelei remained cold-faced, silently watching the furious council members with icy, mocking eyes.

Agatha grinned eerily, while Bruno stood with arms crossed, eyes closed, seemingly indifferent.

"Lance! How did you lead your team?"

Suddenly, an elderly council member turned the blame on Lance.

"Encountering the Team Rocket leader, why didn't you capture him?"

"Yes, how did you let the Team Rocket leader escape, Lance? How could you let him get away?"

"So disappointing, Lance. As the Kanto Champion, are you even qualified? Especially after your opponent left such a taunting message!"

Hearing these accusations, Charles Goodshow's frown deepened.

Lance seemed to only then hear these council members, turning his head slowly, his cold, merciless eyes darkening.

That piercing gaze, not something a human should possess, made those about to join in the blame game feel like they'd fallen into an icy pit, silencing them.

However, the few council members who first criticized Lance hadn't stopped.

"Enough!!"

Charles Goodshow's low growl restored silence to the conference room.

"Don't stop, continue."

But Lance stood up, his dragon-like, sharp eyes overlooking these useless council members, speaking lightly.

Someone as proud as him wouldn't tolerate being berated by these worthless individuals.

Unnoticed, a strong Dragonite had appeared behind Lance, its ominous dragon eyes fixed on the council members.

An invisible pressure filled the room, making some timid members tremble under Dragonite's gaze.

"Useless trash."

Lance sneered.

Being mocked to their faces, these council members were furious but dared not speak out.

Everyone knew Lance's temper, and none dared risk him unleashing Dragonite on them.

"Lance…"

Charles Goodshow sighed.

"Chairman, those Team Rocket rats, I'll wipe them all out."

Lance headed for the conference room door, his cold eyes lingering on the screen, growing darker and more menacing.

"Not a single one left!!"

Lance exited the room, Lorelei adjusted her glasses, also sneering at the council members before leaving.

The rift between the Kanto Elite Four and the Kanto Alliance was widening.

(End of Chapter)

# 86: Chapter 86: Bright Future

Dylan was completely unaware of what was happening at the Indigo Plateau.

Outside of Celadon City, Dylan was bidding farewell to two friends, Leah and Red.

"See you later, Dylan. I'll come visit your Gym when I have time."

"Always welcome. Call me if you need anything."

"Got it."

Leah, full of energy even in disguise, waved enthusiastically at Dylan before walking away cheerfully into the distance.

And then...

Dylan and Red stood there, staring at each other.

"Don't look at me like that. I have to get back to the Gym. Let's have a battle when you come to Rota," Dylan said helplessly after a while.

He could guess that Red really wanted to battle him right now.

Red: (︵)

"Don't make that face. By the way, Red, are you missing any particular type of Pokémon?" Dylan asked, amused by Red's disappointed look.

He wanted to prepare some gifts for Leah and Red. As trainers, their main Pokémon would undoubtedly be the best gift.

After all, Leah and Red had credited him for their achievements in the two Team Rocket incidents. He had to return the favor somehow.

After these two events, Dylan felt he had gained too much with two exceptionally gifted pseudo-legendary Pokémon and an unknown shiny Pokémon.

"No need…"

Red, though shy, wasn't foolish and politely declined with a shake of his head.

"Don't be so polite. I'll pick something for you. Though exceptionally talented pseudo-legendaries are indeed rare, with my dad's connections, we can still find some relatively rare Pokémon."

"..."

Red stared at Dylan's face with his red eyes, hesitated for a while, and then nodded.

"I already have an idea for Leah. What about you, Red?"

"Speaking of which, you're planning to challenge Lance this year, right? If you're going to fight Lance, then a certain type of Pokémon is indispensable!" Dylan thought for a moment and decided to get an Ice-type Pokémon for Red.

Red's battle against Lance at the end of the year would definitely be exciting.

After all, Lance in this world wasn't like the Lance from the first generation of games. Whether Red, who had only trained for a year, could defeat Lance was actually uncertain.

"Let me tell you, although Lance keeps boasting about his invincible Dragon army, his team is actually very weak against Ice types. Trust me."

"If Lance's main lineup hasn't changed much over the past few years, you could sweep through at least two of his Pokémon with a strong Ice-type!" Dylan enthusiastically shared his insights on battling Lance.

Well, although Dylan himself hadn't fought Lance yet.

He wasn't sure about Lance's current main team configuration, but he didn't need to think much. Adding two Ice-types to the team against Lance was definitely a good idea.

"I already have a Lapras."

Red was silent for a moment and then replied.

"Emmm…" Dylan was stunned. True, Red did have a Lapras in the games.

"Then how about a Fairy-type? If you're not planning on a hail team, Alolan Ninetales might not be very suitable for you."

"..." Red blinked, feeling that Dylan's enthusiasm was a bit unusual.

"I have an idea now, Red. Come to Rota Gym when you have time, and we'll have a good battle."

Dylan thought for a moment, and his mind was made up. He smiled at Red.

"Okay." Red nodded.

He wasn't good at socializing and had few people he could truly call friends, but he felt very comfortable spending time with Dylan and Leah.

Leah was outgoing and enthusiastic, while Dylan was gentle and sincere, and a strong rival. Much better than his childhood friend, who couldn't stop complaining the moment they met.

Indeed, childhood friends were just a coincidence.

After saying goodbye to Red, Dylan rode Articuno back to Rota.

Back at the Gym, Dylan contacted Charles Goodshow, who then sent two Pokémon over.

"Dylan, did you receive them?"

"Yes, thank you, President."

Dylan looked at the two shrunken Poké Balls in his hand and smiled.

"No need to thank me. You deserve this, Dylan…" Charles Goodshow's voice on the phone sounded tired and hesitant.

"What's the matter, President?"

"Never mind, it's nothing. Just take a look at those two little ones first." After a moment of silence, Charles Goodshow chuckled.

Is it something troubling? Because Archer and Proton were rescued by Giovanni?

Dylan was curious. As the president of the Kanto League, anything that truly troubled Charles Goodshow wouldn't be simple.

But since Charles Goodshow didn't want to talk about it, there was nothing Dylan could do.

After hanging up with Charles Goodshow, Dylan went to the backyard and released the two Poké Balls.

"You"

"Gha"

Two different cries sounded, and Dylan's gaze first fell on the Pokémon on the ground.

It wasn't very big, only about 0.6 meters tall, looking like a small dinosaur with gray-green skin, a long blunt horn on its head, red eyes, and short arms.

It was Larvitar.

"You?" This Larvitar seemed unafraid of strangers, tilting its head curiously at Dylan.

"Cute little guy." Dylan bent down and patted Larvitar's head, offering it an energy cube.

"You" Larvitar's eyes lit up and it began to munch on the cube.

"Gha" Seeing Larvitar eating so happily, the other Pokémon floated over.

This one had a very strange appearance, with a purple spherical core, large white eyes, and a mouth with fangs. Its body was surrounded by a ring of gray gas.

It was Gastly! And a shiny one at that, as normal Gastly had a black core.

"Didn't expect a shiny Gastly. Did President Goodshow get this from Grandma Agatha?"

Dylan curiously observed the lively shiny Gastly. Agatha had more than one Gengar.

It's possible that one of Agatha's main Gengar's descendants turned out to be a shiny Gastly, and if Charles Goodshow asked for it, he could probably get it.

"Gastly, you have your own food. Don't snatch from Larvitar."

Seeing the shiny Gastly bite into Larvitar's energy cube, and Larvitar glaring at it, Dylan couldn't help but laugh, calming the two little ones.

He took out two more boxes of energy cubes and snacks and placed them in front of them. The shiny Gastly and Larvitar immediately started eating heartily.

With the affinity of his Viridian power and the food, it didn't take long for these two little ones to become close to Dylan.

'Beep~ Gastly, Ghost/Poison type, Gas Pokémon

Ability: Levitate

Moves: Payback, Mean Look, Hypnosis, Lick, Confuse Ray, Perish Song (Egg Move)

Description: Said to be born from gases emitted by graves, Gastly is formed from cursed energies.'

Looking at the shiny Gastly's Pokédex entry, Dylan nodded in satisfaction. It had inherited Perish Song, a move that could be very useful in certain situations.

Dylan then aimed the Pokédex at Larvitar.

'Beep~ Larvitar, Rock/Ground type, Rock Skin Pokémon

Ability: Guts

Moves: Rock Slide, Scary Face, Bite, Payback, Stomp, Dragon Dance (Egg Move)

Description: Larvitar feeds on soil. After eating a large mountain, it will sleep to grow.'

It had inherited Dragon Dance, which was great.

Dylan raised an eyebrow. For Larvitar, which would evolve into Tyranitar, Dragon Dance was an excellent inherited move.

After all, Dragon Dance boosts attack and speed, and since Tyranitar isn't very fast, Dragon Dance is perfect for boosting both speed and attack.

"Excellent, you both have bright futures ahead."

Dylan was very pleased with the two little ones in front of him. A shiny Gengar, especially a Mega Evolved one, could be a top-tier Ghost and Poison-type, and the shiny Mega Gengar was a stunning white, very cool.

And Tyranitar didn't need much explanation. Properly trained, a future Mega Tyranitar would be a dominant Rock-type, a true powerhouse.

It could even rival Darkrai for the top Dark-type spot in his team.

Everyone has a bright future.

(End of Chapter)
